Result: OGBench Scene 上新视角渲染比视频世界模型快 12 倍（RTX 5090 上约 80 FPS），PSNR 最高提升 2.0 dB；8 个任务套件中无模型高斯 token 策略较像素基线最多提升 24.8 个百分点；SplatWAM-P 较 LPWM 提升 12 个百分点、匹配 HIQL 且无需价值函数；LIBERO 上约 1B 模型优于 WorldVLA 与 OpenVLA（约 7B），无大规模机器人预训练时匹配 OpenVLA-OFT。

Conclusion: 以显式 3D 高斯状态为共享表示，可在保证多视角一致性的同时实现高效渲染与强策略性能，验证了在 3D 空间建模世界动力学的有效性。

Abstract: World models typically predict future observations in pixel or latent space, limiting their ability to directly represent true 3D scene evolution. We present SplatWAM (Splatting World Action Model), a generative world model that learns action-conditioned dynamics directly over compact 3D Gaussian primitives. A tokenizer SplatWAM-T first encodes a variable number of unposed views into a fixed budget of Gaussian tokens. A dynamics model SplatWAM-D then predicts future Gaussian states in a single forward pass per action, using log-Euclidean covariance coordinates and differentiable rendering supervision to facilitate learning in an explicit 3D space. Each predicted state can be rasterized from arbitrary viewpoints, enforcing a shared 3D representation across views by construction. On OGBench Scene, SplatWAM renders novel views 12x faster than video world models (~80 FPS on an RTX 5090) with up to 2.0 dB higher PSNR. We further introduce SplatWAM-P, a lightweight flow-matching policy head jointly trained with the dynamics model. Across 8 task suites, model-free Gaussian-token policies outperform pixel-based counterparts by up to 24.8 percentage points. SplatWAM-P improves over Latent Particle World Model (LPWM) by 12 percentage points and matches HIQL without a learned value function. On LIBERO, our ~1B model outperforms WorldVLA (~7B) and the robot-pretrained OpenVLA (~7B), while matching OpenVLA-OFT when both are trained without large-scale robot pretraining. Code and checkpoints will be released.

</details>


### [36] [Streaming Tax: Can Streaming VLMs Replace Their Offline Parents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33474)
*Zhi Yang,Junzhou Chen,Jindong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"流式税"(Streaming Tax)框架，系统评估流式VLM能否在离线任务上替代其离线父模型，发现理解能力保持与实用可替代性之间存在关键差距，并提出免训练插件Stream++。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式VLM（离线VLM的后训练版本）支持实时交互，若能替代离线父模型执行离线任务，可降低部署成本、提升效率；但此前缺乏对其离线任务表现的系统评估。

Method: 提出Streaming Tax评估框架，围绕理解、可信性、泛化三个核心维度，覆盖15个基准进行评测；并通过分析损失来源（历史视觉证据访问受限、无法给出可用响应等）定位差距成因；最后提出免训练插件Stream++。

Result: 发现离线能力保持与实际可替代性之间存在关键差距，且该差距出现在两个阶段：流式适配即使大体保持离线理解能力，也会改变可信行为；原生流式协议进一步带来性能损失。安全方面，流式模型常对有害与合法请求均不作答，而非真正拒绝不安全请求。

Conclusion: 流式VLM尚不能简单替代离线父模型；能力保持不等于可部署替代，需关注可信性与流式协议带来的额外损失。Stream++作为免训练插件可改善流式VLM在离线任务上的表现。

Abstract: Most vision-language models (VLMs) specialize in offline video understanding; in contrast, their post-trained versions, streaming VLMs, can perform live interactions. We investigate whether streaming VLMs can replace their offline parent models on offline tasks. Such replacement could reduce deployment costs and improve efficiency. We propose Streaming Tax to systematically evaluate streaming VLMs on offline tasks. Our exploration spans three core dimensions: understanding, trustworthiness, and generalization across 15 benchmarks. Our findings reveal a critical gap between offline capability preservation and practical replaceability. This gap emerges at two stages: streaming adaptation can alter trustworthy behavior even when offline understanding is largely preserved, while the native streaming protocol can introduce further performance losses. Our analyses show that some of these losses arise from restricted access to historical visual evidence and failures to deliver usable responses. Importantly, regarding safety, streaming models often leave both harmful and legitimate requests unanswered rather than genuinely refusing unsafe ones. We then propose Stream++, a training-free plugin to improve streaming VLMs on offline tasks.

</details>


### [37] [Memory as the Backbone: A Dual-Store Associative Memory Architecture for Visual Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33475)
*Ruizhi Wang,Xiangtao Wang,Bayar Menzat,Jianfeng Wang,Thomas Lukasiewicz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出DuSAM（双存储关联记忆），以显式记忆作为通用视觉骨干网络的组织原则，将图像局部工作记忆与固定容量持久语义记忆耦合，替代自注意力或状态空间模块，在分类、分割、检测任务上取得与同规模骨干相当的性能，并显著提升高分辨率下的吞吐与显存效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索显式记忆能否成为通用视觉骨干的组织原则；受人类认知中工作记忆与长时记忆交互机制启发，希望用可寻址的记忆读写操作替代自注意力/状态空间块，实现上下文计算与语义记忆的联合设计。

Method: 提出DuSAM分层记忆原生骨干：工作记忆（WM）将局部视觉证据组织为紧凑、空间锚定的状态，跨阶段更新与传输，并回读以细化稠密特征；持久语义记忆（PM）为固定容量原型库，汇聚训练图像与标注区域的语义证据成为原型，检索结果增强同一工作状态。以记忆操作替代自注意力或SSM模块，配合UPerNet等下游头进行端到端训练。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K分类、ADE20K语义分割、COCO检测与实例分割上与代表性同规模骨干性能相当；消融显示两个存储均有贡献，PM对标注像素较少的类别组收益尤其明显。2048×2048下完整UPerNet分割器吞吐为DeiT-S/16的2.36倍、显存少59.5%，为线性复杂度VMamba-T的1.24倍。记忆分析显示WM访问具有空间选择性，检索原型与训练区域存在语义关联；类定向移除与恢复表明可在不改变网络参数的情况下通过状态更新补充类别特定经验。

Conclusion: 结果确立显式记忆作为视觉骨干组织原则的可行性，为上下文计算与可寻址语义记忆的联合设计奠定基础。

Abstract: We investigate whether explicit memory can serve as the organizing principle of a general-purpose visual backbone. Inspired by the interaction between working and long-term memory in human cognition, we introduce DuSAM (Dual-Store Associative Memory), a hierarchical memory-native visual backbone coupling image-local working memory (WM) with a fixed-capacity persistent semantic memory (PM). WM organizes local visual evidence into compact, spatially anchored states that are updated and transported across stages, then read back to refine dense features. PM consolidates semantic evidence from training images and annotated regions into prototypes, whose retrieved content enriches the same working states. With these memory operations in place of self-attention or state-space blocks, DuSAM achieves competitive performance relative to representative, similarly sized backbones on ImageNet-1K classification, ADE20K semantic segmentation, and COCO object detection and instance segmentation. Ablations show contributions from both stores, with PM particularly benefiting class groups with fewer annotated training pixels. At $2048\times2048$, the complete DuSAM-based UPerNet segmentor achieves $2.36\times$ the throughput of the evaluated DeiT-S/16 counterpart while using $59.5\%$ less GPU memory, and $1.24\times$ the throughput of the linear-complexity VMamba-T. Memory analyses reveal spatially selective WM access and semantic associations between retrieved prototypes and training regions. Class-targeted removal and restoration further demonstrate that category-specific experience can be replenished through state updates without changing network parameters. These results establish explicit memory as a viable organizing principle for visual backbones and provide a foundation for jointly designing contextual computation and addressable semantic memory.

</details>


### [38] [EvOrderBench: When do sampled frames establish event order?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33492)
*Xiaoqin Feng,Yuqi Li,Xinyuan Li,Neil Rodrigues,Xianglong Wang,Kuiye Ding,Yingli Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EvOrderBench（579 个事件族、真实视频）评测"帧是否足以确定两动作先后"的可回答性，发现开源 VLM 排序准确率接近闭源但拒答正确率≤83%（闭源≥90%），强制选择指标掩盖了这一缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间顺序评测存在两类缺陷：强制选择式基准把所有输入视为可回答，奖励瞎猜；可回答性基准未能区分"帧中缺失某动作"与"帧中两动作均出现但顺序不可辨"两种情况。因此缺少能精细控制帧证据、按标签判定"帧是否确立动作及其顺序"的基准。

Method: 构建 EvOrderBench：每个事件族固定同一视频片段与同一问题，仅改变采样帧。两组四帧输入中仅一组能确立顺序；两组六帧输入在另一组基础上分别追加"能解决顺序"或"不能解决顺序"的帧；对每个输入标注其帧是否确立各动作及二者顺序。评测 16 个开源权重配置与 3 个闭源参考模型。

Result: 在帧足以确立顺序时，最佳开源模型排序准确率接近闭源参考；但在帧不足的输入上，开源模型拒答正确率最高仅 83%，闭源均≥90%，而强制选择准确率无法反映该差距。开源模型主要在"两动作均未出现"时拒答，一旦出现一个或两个动作便倾向强行给出顺序。推理（thinking）可缩小差距，且在同一检查点内启用即有效，其作用主要是区分证据充分与否，而非整体变得更谨慎。

Conclusion: 准确的排序能力并不蕴含对"帧证据不足"的觉察；评测应同时奖励正确排序与适当保留（withholding），而非仅用强制选择准确率衡量时序理解。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are often asked which of two actions in a video happened first, given only a few sampled frames. Such frames can show that both actions occurred without showing which came first, so a reliable model should state an order only when the frames establish it. Forced-choice temporal benchmarks treat every input as answerable and reward lucky guesses, while answerability benchmarks do not separate frames that miss an action from frames that show both actions but not their order. We introduce EvOrderBench, a real-video benchmark of 579 event families. Each family fixes an episode and a question and varies only the frames: of two four-frame inputs, only one establishes the order, and two six-frame inputs extend the other one with either the frames that resolve the order or frames that do not. Every input is labeled for whether its frames establish each action and their order. Across sixteen open-weight configurations and three closed-source references, the best open models order nearly as accurately as the references when the frames establish the order, but withhold correctly on at most 83% of the inputs where they do not, against at least 90% for every reference; forced-choice accuracy cannot reveal this gap. Open models withhold mainly when the frames show neither action; once the frames show one or both, most of them commit to an order. Thinking narrows the gap, even when it is switched on within a single checkpoint, mainly by separating sufficient from insufficient evidence rather than by making models more cautious overall. Accurate ordering thus does not imply knowing when the frames fall short, and benchmarks should reward withholding alongside ordering.

</details>


### [39] [A 3D Simulator for Passive SPAD Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33498)
*Elia Mergaert,Rong Zou,Roberto Pellerito,Simone Nascivera,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个SPAD相机3D仿真管线，联合生成光子探测、相机运动与语义标签，验证零样本仿真到真实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动式SPAD相机适合低光感知，但其真实数据任务标注困难、带标注数据集稀缺，限制了感知模型训练。

Method: 构建可控场景几何与运动的3D仿真管线，耦合传感器成像模型建模光子散粒噪声、暗计数与空间串扰，输出光子探测及运动、语义标签；通过标定光子计数对比以及分类、相机旋转估计、语义分割三项任务的零样本sim-to-real迁移进行评估。

Result: 仅用合成数据训练的分类器在<2 lux真实低光记录上高精度；合成相机扫掠训练的旋转估计模型零样本用于真实20°、30°扫掠，在0.5与5.0 lux下平均绝对旋转误差<1°；合成稠密标签训练的语义分割模型可直接推理真实数据。

Conclusion: 该仿真器可作为真实SPAD感知的实用标注训练数据来源，稠密合成监督对真实低光感知有效。

Abstract: Passive single-photon avalanche diode (SPAD) cameras provide short-exposure observations for low-light perception, but collecting task annotations for these sensors remains difficult, and annotated datasets are scarce. We present a 3D simulation pipeline that generates realistic photon detections together with camera-motion and semantic labels, enabling perception models to be trained on synthetic SPAD observations and applied directly to real-world data. The pipeline couples controllable scene geometry and motion with a sensor-informed observation model that accounts for photon shot noise, dark counts, and spatial crosstalk. We evaluate the pipeline through calibrated photon-count comparisons and zero-shot sim-to-real transfer across three downstream perception tasks. We show that classifiers trained only on synthetic SPAD data achieve high accuracy on real low-light recordings at illumination levels less than 2 lux, without real-data fine-tuning. Similarly, we apply models trained entirely on simulated camera sweeps to real-world camera-rotation estimation and achieve mean absolute rotation errors of less than 1 degree on real 20-degree and 30-degree sweeps at 0.5 and 5.0 lux, when deployed zero-shot. We additionally train semantic segmentation models on synthetic data with automatically generated dense labels and perform direct inference on real-world low-light recordings; the results further demonstrate the usefulness of synthetic dense supervision for real low-light perception. Our simulator thus provides a practical source of annotated training data for real SPAD perception.

</details>


### [40] [Separating Environment and Sensor Shift from Acquisition Protocol in Physical Vision Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33507)
*Yuyang Yuan,Jason Wu,Mohammadali Khodabandehlou,Jianpeng Yao,Hang Qiu,Mani Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文重新构建了物理基准ImageNet-ES-Diverse的四条受控采集流程（改变打印图像尺度与相机变焦），并加入真实物体初步研究，发现采集协议选择显著改变基准难度，原基准上Lens相对自动曝光的平均精度优势不复现，但oracle曝光选择仍有显著增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实部署中视觉系统需适应光照变化等环境条件，已有工作提出按环境和下游任务自适应选择相机参数（如曝光）的策略，但评估这类策略依赖可靠的物理基准；作者质疑现有基准的采集协议是否稳健、结论是否可复现。

Method: 针对评估自适应曝光控制策略Lens的物理基准ImageNet-ES-Diverse（在不同光照下重拍打印的ImageNet图像），作者构建四条受控采集流程，系统改变打印图像尺度与相机变焦，并开展以直接拍摄真实物体为对象的初步研究，在多个下游基线上比较Lens、自动曝光与oracle曝光选择。

Result: 采集选择在所有下游基线上都显著改变基准难度；原基准上Lens相对自动曝光的平均分类精度优势在作者采集的各流程及真实物体初步研究中均未持续出现；与此同时，oracle曝光选择仍保持可观增益，说明传感器控制仍具潜力。

Conclusion: 采集协议是物理基准评估中的重要影响因素，评估自适应感知收益时应明确报告协议，并在多种采集设置下交叉验证，以避免结论对采集方式的过度依赖。

Abstract: In real-world deployments, vision systems must operate under varying environmental conditions, such as illumination changes. Recent work has explored adaptive sensing strategies that select camera parameters according to the current environment and downstream task to improve task performance. Reliable physical benchmarks are therefore essential for evaluating such strategies. We study ImageNet-ES-Diverse, a physical benchmark that evaluates the adaptive exposure control strategy Lens by recapturing printed ImageNet images under varying illumination conditions. We construct four controlled acquisition pipelines that vary printed-image scale and camera zoom, and conduct a pilot study with directly captured physical objects. We find that the acquisition choices substantially change benchmark difficulty on all downstream baselines. Moreover, the average classification-accuracy advantage of Lens over auto exposure observed on the original benchmark does not persist in either our collected pipelines or real-object pilot study. Oracle exposure selection nevertheless retains substantial gains, indicating remaining potential for sensor control. These findings highlight acquisition protocol as an important factor in physical benchmark evaluation and motivate explicit protocol reporting and evaluation across multiple acquisition setups when assessing adaptive-sensing benefits.

</details>


### [41] [ArtLang-4D: Articulation-Aware Dynamic Language Fields from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33511)
*Sylvia Yuan,Dan Wang,Ravi Ramamoorthi,Xinrui Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ArtLang-4D 从单目 RGB-D 视频重建铰接物体，并用"关节状态时间 t"（而非视频时间）索引动态语言场，实现按部件与状态联合检索及语言指令执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 语言场每点只挂一个特征，无法区分"抽屉拉到最外/门开一半"这类同一部件的不同状态描述；4D 语言场以视频时间条件化，视频时间无运动学含义且用户无法指定。

Method: 几何采用规范三角面片场，面片沿从视频中自动发现（无需关节初始化）的带类型螺旋关节运动；语言场由带 t 标签的描述比较性状态短语的字幕库监督，嵌入经白化的开放词表文本空间；自由文本查询通过在部件与状态上联合最大化相关性作答，同时返回部件与状态，再沿恢复的关节驱动该部件执行指令。

Result: 在 Articulate-100 的 100 个物体上，关节与部件几何精度优于重建与铰接化基线；状态检索可靠性显著高于状态无关语言场；语言指令执行的运动误差更低。

Conclusion: 以关节状态为索引变量的语言场能有效桥接开放词表描述与铰接物体操作，为单目视频下的语言引导铰接物体交互提供可行方案。

Abstract: Language that describes an articulated object is often about its state: the drawer pulled out the furthest, the door opened halfway, push the drawer all the way in. Existing 3D language fields attach one feature to each point and cannot tell these descriptions apart, and 4D language fields condition on video time, which has no kinematic meaning and cannot be set by a user. We present ArtLang-4D, which reconstructs an articulated object from a single monocular RGB-D video and equips it with a dynamic language field indexed by articulation time t, the normalized state of its joints, instead of video time. The geometry is a canonical triangle field whose triangles follow typed screw joints, discovered from the video without any joint initialization. The language field is supervised by a t-tagged caption bank that describes each part with comparative state phrases in a whitened open-vocabulary text space. A free-form query is answered by maximizing part relevance jointly over parts and states, which returns both the part and the articulation state it describes; a command is then executed by moving only that part along its recovered joint. On the 100 objects of Articulate-100, ArtLang-4D recovers more accurate joints and part geometry than reconstruction and articulation baselines, retrieves the queried state far more reliably than a state-agnostic language field, and executes language commands with lower motion error.

</details>


### [42] [AVERT: Anomalous Value Erasure with Response Testing against Visual Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33532)
*Newt Hue-Nam K. Nguyen,Jui-Yu Ho,Wei-Han Chen,Pin-Yen Ting,Yangqiu Song,Shang-Tse Chen,Jun-Cheng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AVERT通过"仅在答案依赖对抗特征方向时才移除该方向"来防御视觉提示注入，将攻击成功率从86-90%降至≤0.6%，同时保留≥96%的良性效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御（扩散净化、图像token掩码）在缓解视觉提示注入时严重损害模型在良性输入上的效用，需要一种兼顾防御与效用的方法。

Method: 识别异常值子空间：将各输入注意力头输出的统计量与干净图像比较，提取偏离最大的方向；检测答案依赖：仅对依赖这些方向的答案（移除方向后似然崩塌，或在无关问题下与用户问题下似然相近）进行投影移除。

Result: 在4个VLM（Qwen2-VL、Qwen2.5-VL、LLaVA、InternVL2.5）和4个注入基准的白盒攻击下，平均攻击成功率从86-90%降至最多0.6%，在13个基准上平均保留至少96%的未防御效用；防御-效用F-score为98.0-99.1，在所有模型上均优于对比防御方法。

Conclusion: 条件化地移除对抗特征方向可在几乎不损失良性效用的前提下高效防御视觉提示注入，实现防御与效用的良好平衡。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) are vulnerable to visual prompt injection, in which an adversarially perturbed image forces the model to respond with the attacker's chosen content, such as malicious tool calls, website links, or advertisements, sometimes regardless of the user's question. Existing defenses, such as diffusion-based purification and image-token masking, mitigate these attacks at a substantial cost to the model's utility on benign inputs. To address this challenge, we propose AVERT, which removes adversarial feature directions only when the model's answer depends on them. Our key procedures involve identifying anomalous value subspaces and detecting answer dependence. To identify the anomalous value subspace, we compare the statistics of each input's attention-head outputs with those of clean images and extract the directions along which the input deviates the most. We then project out these directions to remove the injected content. Because the same directions also carry benign visual information, we apply the removal only to answers that depend on them, namely answers whose likelihood collapses once the directions are removed or that are as likely under unrelated questions as under the user's question. Against white-box attacks on four VLMs (Qwen2-VL, Qwen2.5-VL, LLaVA, InternVL2.5) and four injection benchmarks, AVERT lowers average attack success from 86-90% to at most 0.6% while retaining at least 96% of undefended utility averaged over thirteen benchmarks. Its defense-utility F-score ($F_{DU}$), the harmonic mean of attacks stopped and utility retained, is 98.0-99.1 and the highest among all compared defenses on every model.

</details>


### [43] [Frame geometry, fascicle extrapolation and leaderboard overfitting in muscle ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33539)
*Soheil Kooklan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究在UMUD挑战赛单一提交中自动测量羽状角、肌束长度与肌肉厚度，发现帧几何与肌束延伸规则对读数的影响堪比模型选择，而在约100帧公开榜上做的微调无法迁移到私有榜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B超自动化读数依赖存储帧的几何参数及肌束离图后的延伸方式，且挑战赛提交常基于小规模公开榜调参；作者希望量化每一项决策（尤其是帧几何与延伸规则）对最终得分与读数的实际影响，并检验公开榜微调能否泛化。

Method: 在UMUD挑战赛单次提交中：按屏幕布局设定比例与像素纵横比，将每帧重采样为方形像素，用U-Net分割腱膜与肌束片段，再几何计算三项读数；角度与长度与公开notebook融合。利用加性评分设计，以13个基提交产生65组单变量对比，在公开/私有划分上分别评估每项决策；并对UMUD专家基准进行组织重叠审计。

Result: 主提交私有榜得分0.27608（690队中第69）。全帧方形像素读取公开榜损失0.029、私有榜0.037。20项后期修正中仅9项在私有榜保持效应方向。专家基准35帧中26帧组织与DL_Track_US及挑战训练图像重叠；在9帧无匹配样本上，方法得分0.213，两组专家互评0.218，DL_Track_US 0.263（严格双评审条件分别为0.209、0.196、0.259），差异未确立。

Conclusion: 帧几何与肌束延伸规则对读数的影响与模型选择相当；基于约100帧公开榜的小幅调参不具备迁移性。自动读数评估需报告几何处理与数据集泄漏情况，并采用更大的验证集。

Abstract: Automated readings of pennation angle, fascicle length and muscle thickness from B-mode ultrasound depend on the geometry of the stored frame and on how fascicles that leave the image are extended, and challenge entries add settings chosen on a small public leaderboard. This study measures all three in one entry of the UMUD Challenge, which scored 309 frames from six screen layouts, 103 on a public and 206 on a private leaderboard. The pipeline applies layout-specific values of scale and pixel aspect, resamples each frame to square pixels, segments aponeuroses and fascicle fragments with U-Nets and measures the three readings geometrically; the submitted entry blended its angle and length with public notebooks. Because the score is additive, 65 single-change contrasts from 13 base entries measured each decision on both splits. The primary selected entry scored 0.27608 on the private split, the 69th reported position of 690 teams. Reading every frame with square pixels cost 0.029 public and 0.037 private. Of 20 late-stage corrections, 9 kept the sign of their effect on the private split. An audit of the UMUD expert benchmark found that 26 of its 35 frames share their tissue with training images of both DL_Track_US and the challenge. On the 9 frames with no detected match in either set or orientation, the recipe scored 0.213, two experts against two others 0.218 and DL_Track_US 0.263 (0.209, 0.196 and 0.259 when both raters of a reference pair are required), with no difference established. Frame geometry and the extension rule moved readings as much as the choice of model, and small corrections tuned on about 100 public frames did not carry over.

</details>


### [44] [EgoCARBS: Egocentric Video Meets Active Acoustics for Kitchen Action Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33565)
*Saif Mahmud,Vineet Parikh,Bharath Hariharan,Cheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出EgoCARBS多模态数据集与基准：15名参与者在自宅厨房烹饪，头戴摄像头眼镜+智能手表主动声学感知，与自我中心/非自我中心视频及手表音频、IMU同步，含4,019条动词-名词动作标注、11.4小时；研究显示可穿戴主动声学在动词识别上可提供不亚于甚至超过外置摄像头的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心视频只记录“看什么”，常丢失身体动作（手出画、手臂运动不可见）；外置摄像头可补足上下文但需部署在活动场所。主动声学感知可由可穿戴设备自身从回声恢复手、臂及周边物体运动，但缺少与自我中心视频配对的公开数据集。

Method: 1) 构建EgoCARBS数据集：15名参与者、真实家庭厨房、无脚本烹饪；佩戴摄像头眼镜与商用智能手表，二者均具主动声学感知（扬声器发出不可听chirp、麦克风接收回声）；同步采集自我中心与非自我中心视频、手表音频与IMU；标注4,019条verb-noun动作。2) 对比研究可穿戴信号对自我中心视频的增益，含动词识别消融，并设定trimmed动作识别、检测与预测任务。

Result: 动词识别上，对训练中见过的参与者，可穿戴信号带来的增益至少与外置摄像头相当；对未见过的参与者增益更大，而外置摄像头对新人无帮助。该增益主要来自主动声学本身，并在摄像头看不到手部时贡献最大。Trimmed动作识别、检测与预测仍为开放问题。

Conclusion: 主动声学可穿戴感知是自我中心视觉的有效补充，且无需环境部署、对未见用户具泛化优势。作者将公开数据、采集与标注工具及预训练模型，推动后续研究。

Abstract: Egocentric video records what a person looks at, but often misses what their body does: the hands leave the frame, and arm motion goes unseen. An exocentric camera recovers this context, but it must be installed wherever the activity happens. Active acoustic sensing can recover it from the wearables themselves: a device's speaker emits inaudible chirps, and its microphone senses the motion of the hands, arms, and nearby objects from their echoes. Yet no public dataset pairs it with egocentric video. We present EgoCARBS, with two deliverables. The first is a multimodal dataset and benchmark: 15 participants cook unscripted meals in their own kitchens wearing camera glasses and an off-the-shelf smartwatch that both sense with active acoustics, synchronized with egocentric and exocentric video and the watch's audio and IMU, with 4,019 verb-noun action annotations in 11.4 hours. The second is a study of what the wearables add to egocentric video. On verb recognition, they add at least as much as an exocentric camera for participants seen in training, and even more for new participants, for whom the exocentric camera does not help. Active acoustics alone provides most of this gain, and helps most where the camera does not see the hands. Trimmed action recognition, detection, and anticipation remain open. We will release the data, our data collection and annotation tools, and pre-trained models.

</details>


### [45] [ProPolar: Progressive Polar Decomposition for Implicit Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33567)
*Pureum Kim,Younggeon Ryu,Dongyoon Lee,Hae Beom Lee,Kyong Hwan Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对INR"先全局低频、后局部高频"的学习规律，标准优化器对此无感知，会积累与主导低频方向错位的动量分量。作者提出子空间动量优化框架，加入渐进增长秩调度器与秩感知学习率调度器：训练中扩张低秩动量子空间，并在秩增长阶段维持峰值学习率。在图像拟合、SISR、NeRF上收敛更快、重建质量更高，增益集中在高频细化阶段；NeRF超参搜索下的对照实验验证增益稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: INR训练呈现从全局低频结构到局部高频细节的粗到细演化，但标准优化器（如带动量/自适应方法）与该行为解耦：动量缓冲中累积的梯度矩阵会保留在早期与主导低频方向错位的分量，影响后期高频细化。作者希望让优化器的动量空间随训练阶段自适应演化，以匹配这一频谱演化规律。

Method: 在低秩子空间内更新动量矩阵的子空间动量优化基础上，引入两个组件：(1) 渐进增长秩调度器，随训练推进逐步扩大动量子空间的秩；(2) 秩感知学习率调度器，在秩增长期间保持峰值学习率，使新增子空间能够有效参与细粒度细节的恢复。

Result: 在图像拟合、单图像超分辨率（SISR）与神经辐射场优化三类任务上，相比有竞争力的优化器收敛更快、峰值重建质量更高；最大增益出现在高频细化阶段，与秩调度所针对的粗到细进程相吻合。针对NeRF的超参数优化（HPO）研究进一步表明，在匹配的搜索预算下增益依然保持。

Conclusion: 通过将动量子空间的秩增长与学习率调度耦合到INR的粗到细学习进程，可有效抑制早期错位动量分量的干扰，提升高频细节恢复与最终重建质量，且改进在受控HPO条件下依然成立。

Abstract: Implicit neural representations (INRs) capture global low-frequency structure during the early stage of training and then refine localized high-frequency details, afterwards. However, standard optimizers are agnostic to the coarse-to-fine learning behavior of INRs. Such optimizers accumulate a gradient matrix in momentum buffer and retain components misaligned with the dominant low-frequency directions in the early stage. We propose a progressively growing rank scheduler and a rank-aware learning-rate scheduler for enhancing a subspace-based momentum optimization, where a momentum matrix is updated within a low-rank subspace. The rank schedule expands the subspace during training, and the scheduler holds the peak learning rate through rank growth so that the added subspace contributes to fine-detail recovery. Experiments on image fitting, single-image super-resolution (SISR), and neural radiance field optimization show that the proposed method improves convergence and peak reconstruction quality over competitive optimizers. The largest gains appear in the high-frequency refinement stage, mirroring the coarse-to-fine progression that the rank schedule targets. A significant study on hyperparameter optimization (HPO) on NeRF further confirms that these gains persist under matched search budgets.

</details>


### [46] [DoseSteer: Steering diffusion-based dose prediction models towards clinical targets via Dual-Response Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33577)
*Shupeng Cheng,Jing Jia,Jincheng Wu,Zhangyu Wang,Gao Wang,Maohe Jiang,Chi Xu,Feiyang Wang,Zhilei Liu Shen,Guanyang Wang,Ruishan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DoseSteer推理时框架，通过无需重训的方式引导冻结的扩散式剂量预测模型满足结构级临床剂量目标，其核心算法Dual-Response Guidance（DRG）联合求解各结构的引导强度，在5种配置上显著降低目标误差与剂量误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI剂量预测模型虽能由解剖结构生成3D剂量分布，但常偏离结构级临床剂量目标；部署后缺乏直接纠正机制，只能将目标约束推给下游计划优化。

Method: DoseSteer为推理时框架，对冻结的扩散式剂量预测模型施加引导而无需重训。核心为Dual-Response Guidance（DRG）：以结构为中心，探测模型对各结构噪声剂量与上下文扰动的响应，并联立求解逐结构引导强度，在满足临床目标的同时保持整体剂量预测质量。

Result: 在覆盖3种扩散架构与3个数据集的5种数据集-模型配置上，DRG在相同参考目标代理下优于12个免训练基线：至少消除97%的目标误差，官方剂量误差平均降低7.8%，并改善未引导结构之外的剂量保真度。

Conclusion: DoseSteer在测试阶段调和了学习到的解剖剂量先验与显式结构级目标，提供了在计划优化前精炼AI剂量预测的有原则的免训练机制。

Abstract: AI dose-prediction models generate patient-specific 3D dose distributions from patient anatomy. Yet these predictions can still miss structure-level clinical dose targets. Once deployed, most existing predictors offer no direct mechanism to correct such deviations, deferring target enforcement to downstream plan optimization. We introduce DoseSteer, an inference-time framework that steers frozen diffusion-based dose prediction models towards specified clinical targets without retraining. To meet clinical targets while preserving overall dose prediction quality, we propose Dual-Response Guidance (DRG), a structure-centric algorithm that probes the model's responses to perturbations in each structure's noisy dose and context and jointly solves for per-structure steering strengths. Across five dataset-model configurations spanning three diffusion architectures and three datasets, DRG outperforms 12 training-free baselines under identical reference-derived target proxies, removing at least 97% of the target error, reducing the official dose error by 7.8% on average, and improving dose fidelity outside the steered structures. DoseSteer reconciles learned anatomical dose priors with explicit structure-level targets at test time, establishing a principled, training-free mechanism to refine AI dose predictions prior to plan optimization. Code is available at https://github.com/csp2002/DoseSteer.

</details>


### [47] [LLMVC: Large Language Model-Guided Generative Video Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33580)
*Lebin Zhou,Zhenghao Chen,Nam Ling,Wei Wang,Wei Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LLMVC：利用冻结的Video-LLM生成紧凑语义token，作为文本侧信道引导视频神经压缩的编解码与熵编码，在LPIPS/DISTS上优于传统与近期神经视频压缩方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式视频压缩需将高层语义与稠密视觉细节混压进统一隐空间，导致熵编码/解码复杂化，且在激进压缩下出现语义漂移。

Method: 用冻结Video-LLM的总结能力生成与时间隐切片对齐的紧凑语义token；设计Hybrid Bidirectional Bimodal Attention（HBBA）实现视觉与语义特征在编解码中的双向交互；设计Semantic-Conditioned Cross-Attention（SCCA）将语义特征注入熵编码的概率建模；语义特征经文本侧信道传输。

Result: 在UVG、MCL-JCV、HEVC上，LLMVC在LPIPS与DISTS指标上稳定优于传统及近期神经视频压缩方法，保真度也优于近期感知编解码器。

Conclusion: 以语义token作为高层引导可改善表示质量与语义一致性，兼顾感知与保真性能。

Abstract: Recent generative video methods achieve strong perceptual compression by encoding videos into compact latent representations and reconstructing them with powerful generative decoders. However, visual encoders must compress both high-level semantics and dense visual details into a unified latent space, which complicates entropy coding and decoding and may cause semantic drift under aggressive compression. In this work, we propose Large Language Model-Guided Video Compression (LLMVC), which leverages the summarization capability of frozen video large language models (Video-LLMs) to generate compact semantic tokens aligned with temporal latent slices for guiding video compression. Specifically, Hybrid Bidirectional Bimodal Attention (HBBA) enables bidirectional interactions between visual and semantic features during encoding and decoding. Semantic-Conditioned Cross-Attention (SCCA) incorporates semantic features into probability modeling during entropy coding. These semantic features provide high-level guidance throughout the pipeline, improving representation quality and encouraging semantic consistency through a transmitted text side channel. Experiments on UVG, MCL-JCV, and HEVC show that LLMVC consistently improves compression performance over traditional and recent neural video compression methods in terms of LPIPS and DISTS, while also achieving better fidelity than recent perceptual codecs.

</details>


### [48] [SEEL: Towards End-to-End 3D Sign Generation with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33588)
*Ronglai Zuo,Fei Ni,Junpeng Jing,Rolandos Alexandros Potamias,Jiankang Deng,Stefanos Zafeiriou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SEEL端到端框架，通过对齐分词与生成目标缓解两阶段流水线的不匹配问题，在三个数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"运动分词器+语言模型"的两阶段流水线目标不一致：分词器孤立地最小化重建误差，生成器却依赖刚性one-hot参考token训练；直接端到端引入STE会产生有偏梯度与码本坍塌。

Method: 两个互补机制：(1) 重建感知生成——在上下文中解码候选token，将其3D运动误差转为软目标，以物理运动质量引导生成器；(2) 生成感知分词——将连续码本期望传入冻结的生成器，把生成运动梯度回传至分词器，避免码本坍塌。

Result: 分词器与生成器得以协同演化，在CSL-Daily、How2Sign和Phoenix-2014T上达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 以运动质量为目标对齐分词与生成，可在无需STE的情况下实现有效的端到端文本到3D手语生成。

Abstract: 3D sign language generation aims to produce signing motion from written text. Motivated by the linguistic nature of sign language, recent methods combine a motion tokenizer with a language model to predict motion tokens from text. However, both components are optimized through a two-stage pipeline with mismatched objectives: the tokenizer minimizes reconstruction error in isolation, while the generator learns from rigid one-hot reference tokens. A straightforward end-to-end extension bridges discrete token selection with straight-through estimators (STE), suffering from biased gradients and codebook collapse. To address these limitations, we propose SEEL, an end-to-end framework that aligns tokenization and generation with motion quality through two complementary mechanisms. First, to overcome rigid one-hot supervision, reconstruction-aware generation decodes candidate tokens in context and converts their 3D motion errors into soft targets, guiding the generator with physical motion quality. Second, to propagate generation feedback without STE, generation-aware tokenization passes continuous codebook expectations through the frozen generator, routing generated-motion gradients to the tokenizer to prevent codebook collapse. Combining both mechanisms enables the tokenizer and generator to co-evolve, achieving state-of-the-art performance on CSL-Daily, How2Sign, and Phoenix-2014T. Code and models will be released to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [49] [VivaWorld: Long-Horizon Video World Model With Compositional 3D Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33591)
*Ying Jiang,Donglai Xiang,Anita Hu,Masha Shugrina*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VivaWorld 从单张图像生成几何一致、视角可控的长视频：构建点云+纹理网格的混合3D表示，配合连通域过滤与全局/物体锚定混合相机嵌入，经控制适配器注入视频生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程视频生成对交互式世界建模、物理智能与空间推理至关重要，但缺乏3D grounding 时难以保持几何一致性与精确相机控制。

Method: 1) 组合式混合3D表示：重建点云保留观测场景几何与外观，生成式纹理网格提供360°新视角下的完整物体几何；2) 连通区域过滤（connected-region filter）去除不一致的悬浮组件，提升跨视角重建鲁棒性；3) 混合相机嵌入：结合全局与物体锚定的 Plücker 射线，同时刻画场景级相机运动与相机-物体关系；4) 通过控制适配器将相机嵌入与视觉引导一同注入视频生成模型。

Result: 大量实验表明达到 SOTA 的视觉质量与相机控制能力，并支持可控新视角合成、长时程场景探索及物理AI应用。

Conclusion: 以混合3D表示作为几何先验、辅以混合相机嵌入的可控长视频生成框架，可在单图输入下实现几何一致、视角可控的长时程视频生成。

Abstract: Long-horizon video generation is key to interactive world modeling, physical intelligence, and spatial reasoning, yet maintaining geometric consistency and accurate camera control remains challenging without 3D grounding. We introduce \textbf{VivaWorld}, a framework for generating geometry- and view-consistent long videos from an image. We first construct a compositional hybrid 3D representation that combines reconstructed point clouds, which preserve observed scene geometry and appearance, with generated textured meshes, which provide complete object geometry across $360^\circ$ novel views. We also introduce a connected-region filter removing inconsistent floating components for robust cross-view reconstruction and propose a hybrid camera embedding that combines global and object-anchored Plücker rays to capture both scene-level camera motion and camera-object relationships, and inject it together with the visual guidance into a video generation model via a control adapter. Extensive experiments show VivaWorld achieves state-of-the-art visual quality and camera control, while supporting controllable novel-view synthesis, long-horizon scene exploration, and physical AI. Project page is available at: https://research.nvidia.com/labs/sil/projects/vivaworld/

</details>


### [50] [What Is Redundant at High Resolution? Efficient Attention and Feed-Forward Design for All-in-One Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33636)
*Dongheon Lee,DongJae Lee,Sewhan Chun,Chanseul Cho,Youngwook Kim,Youngmin Ro,Yonghyun Jeong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiGLR 提出一种高效多尺度一体化图像复原网络，通过分析自注意力与 ConvFFN 的空间冗余，设计学习式输入无关核、块路由混合注意力与双空间 FFN，在保持SOTA性能的同时实现最高 4.24× 推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多尺度 AIO-IR 在高分辨率阶段处理大空间特征图导致计算开销与内存访问成本高，HR 高效处理困难。

Method: 基于空间冗余分析识别三类冗余（最细尺度逐查询读出、中间尺度相邻查询键路由冗余、ConvFFN扩展空间中的空间混合），分别以：学习式输入无关核替代最细尺度自注意力；Block-Routed Hybrid Attention（粗尺度稠密注意力＋基于value残差的细尺度块稀疏注意力，路由索引跨相邻查询共享）；Dual-Space FFN（紧凑空间做空间混合、扩展空间做逐点非线性，支持算子融合）。

Result: 取得 SOTA 性能，相比现有方法最高 4.24× 推理加速。

Conclusion: 将冗余消除与网络结构设计结合，可在多尺度 AIO-IR 中实现精度与效率的良好权衡。

Abstract: Multi-scale architectures are widely adopted in all-in-one image restoration (AIO-IR) to capture larger-scale context at low-resolution stages and refine details at high-resolution (HR) stages. However, processing spatially large feature maps at HR stages incurs substantial computational costs and memory traffic, making efficient HR processing challenging. To address this challenge, we propose High-resolution Global-Local Restoration (HiGLR), an efficient multi-scale AIO-IR network grounded in an analysis of spatial redundancy in self-attention and convolutional feed-forward networks (ConvFFNs). Specifically, this analysis identifies three key redundancies: per-query readout at the finest-scale stage, key routing across neighboring queries at intermediate stages, and spatial mixing in the expanded feature spaces of ConvFFNs. Accordingly, HiGLR replaces costly self-attention at the finest-scale stage with a learned input-independent kernel. At intermediate stages, we propose a Block-Routed Hybrid Attention, which combines coarse-scale dense attention with fine-scale block-sparse attention over value residuals relative to coarse summaries, and shares routing indices across neighboring queries. We further introduce a Dual-Space FFN that performs spatial mixing in a compact space and pointwise nonlinear transformation in an expanded space, enabling efficient kernel fusion. Combining these designs, HiGLR achieves state-of-the-art performance while delivering up to a $4.24\times$ inference speedup over existing methods.

</details>


### [51] [CariesBench: A Hierarchical Multi-view Benchmark for Dental Caries Screening and Diagnostic Reasoning from 3D Intraoral Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33643)
*Xuefen Liu,Yanxiong,Xinquan Yang,Fangqian Zhang,Qinyi Lu,Yuning Zhang,Xianxu Hou,Shinan Zhang,Wen Ma,Linlin Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CariesBench：基于 5,932 例 3D 口内扫描构建的层级化龋病评估基准，含 85,455 张标准化多视角渲染图和 133,994 条专家质控 VQA 实例；并微调得 IntraoralGPT，19 项任务宏平均准确率由 52.7% 升至 69.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 龋病评估是结构化临床流程，需完成病变定位、牙位与牙面识别及严重程度分级，而非图像级识别。3D 口内扫描（IOS）保留牙体几何与表面形态，可支持互补视角标准化渲染，但由此产生"解剖学基础的多视角推理"需求。现有多模态大模型与牙科视觉语言基准无法同时评估牙弓—牙—牙面层级推理、跨视角证据整合与牙面级龋病判断，存在评测空白。

Method: 构建 CariesBench：源数据 5,932 例 3D IOS，统一渲染为 85,455 张多视角图像，经专家质控生成 133,994 条临床依据驱动 VQA 实例；任务分为牙弓级临床筛查（ACS）、牙级诊断推理（TDR）、结构化临床决策（SCD）三类互补任务族。并用其训练集微调 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 得到 IntraoralGPT；评测 24 个代表性基线 MLLM。

Result: 24 个基线模型普遍呈现牙弓级筛查（ACS）准确率高于牙级诊断推理（TDR）的基准层面性能落差，显示层级推理与跨视角整合是主要瓶颈。领域监督显著提升基线：19 任务宏平均临床准确率 52.7%→69.5%，TDR Overall 34.4%→44.7%。

Conclusion: CariesBench 填补了层级化、多视角、牙面级龋病评估基准的空白，表明现有 MLLM 在细粒度诊断推理上仍明显不足，而针对性领域监督可带来可观增益，为牙科多模态模型的可解释、结构化评测提供基础。

Abstract: Dental caries assessment is a structured clinical process requiring lesion localization, identification of the affected tooth and surface, and severity grading rather than simple image-level recognition. 3D intraoral scans (IOS) preserve detailed dental geometry and surface morphology and support standardized rendering from complementary viewpoints, enabling fine-grained visual assessment while introducing a distinct need for anatomically grounded multi-view reasoning. Despite recent advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and dental vision-language benchmarks, existing evaluations do not jointly assess hierarchical arch-to-tooth-to-surface reasoning, cross-view evidence integration, and surface-level caries assessment. To address this gap, we introduce CariesBench, a hierarchical benchmark constructed from 5,932 3D IOS, comprising 85,455 standardized multi-view renderings and 133,994 clinically grounded visual question answering instances with expert quality control. CariesBench comprises three complementary task families: Arch-level Clinical Screening (ACS), Tooth-level Diagnostic Reasoning (TDR), and Structured Clinical Decision-Making (SCD). Using the CariesBench training set, we further fine-tune Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct to obtain IntraoralGPT. Extensive evaluation of 24 representative baseline MLLMs reveals a substantial benchmark-level performance disparity between arch-level screening and tooth-level diagnostic reasoning, with higher accuracy on ACS than on TDR. CariesBench-specific supervision substantially improves the Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct baseline, increasing the 19-task macro-average clinical accuracy from 52.7% to 69.5% and TDR Overall from 34.4% to 44.7%.

</details>


### [52] [Linear-Time Online Visual Geometry Transformer with Hybrid Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33650)
*Haoyu Kang,Pingzhi Li,Ruichen Zhang,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LinearVGGT：混合Mamba2与稀疏注意力的线性复杂度流式3D重建模型，蒸馏自StreamVGGT，实现近常数延迟与精度超越教师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer式3D重建（VGGT、π³）及因果注意力流式模型（StreamVGGT）需缓存全部历史token，总计算量随流长呈二次增长；而CUT3R、TTT3R等循环模型虽为常数开销但会渐进遗忘早期几何。需在长序列场景下兼顾效率与长程几何回忆。

Method: 提出LinearVGGT混合模型：每四个全局层中的三层采用Mamba2进行高效序列建模，剩余层使用寄存器引导的稀疏注意力检索少量相关历史帧，以弥补Mamba2精确长程召回不足；模型由StreamVGGT蒸馏得到。

Result: 蒸馏仅需StreamVGGT学习VGGT所用GPU小时的9%、训练帧数的31%；1,024帧下延迟近乎恒定，约27 ms/帧，平均比StreamVGGT快12×；精度超过其教师模型，并与SOTA流式模型LingBot-Map相当，同时快2.5×、内存少2.7×。

Conclusion: 以Mamba2为主干、稀疏注意力为补充的混合架构可在流式3D重建中同时实现线性复杂度、近常数延迟与高精度，且蒸馏成本低，具备实用性。

Abstract: Transformer-based 3D reconstruction models such as VGGT and $π^3$ predict cameras, depth and point maps directly from many input views. Causal attention models such as StreamVGGT extend them to online video but cache all past tokens, so their total compute grows quadratically with the stream length. Recurrent models such as CUT3R and TTT3R keep a fixed-size state at constant cost, but they progressively forget early geometry. We present LinearVGGT, a hybrid model distilled from StreamVGGT, which uses Mamba2 layers for efficient sequence modeling in three of every four global layers. The remaining layers use register-guided sparse attention to retrieve a few relevant past frames, compensating for the limited exact long-range recall of Mamba2. Our distillation needs only 9\% of the GPU hours and 31\% of the training frames that StreamVGGT uses to learn from VGGT. LinearVGGT keeps an almost constant latency of 27 ms per frame over 1,024 frames and is on average $12 \times$ faster than StreamVGGT. It outperforms its teacher and is competitive with the state-of-the-art streaming model LingBot-Map, while running $2.5\times$ faster and using $2.7\times$ less memory.

</details>


### [53] [Fine-Grained Ego-Vehicle Action Recognition from Driving Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33654)
*Zhipeng Bao,Wenjie Zhao,Qianwen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出EgoManeuver统一基准与DenseEgo基线模型，用于细粒度自车动作识别，并验证其在驾驶视频检索中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模驾驶视频缺乏显式自车动作标签，且现有数据集在动作定义、标注粒度和监督信号上不一致，难以支持关键机动发现与自动驾驶模型的系统开发评估。

Method: 构建EgoManeuver：基于七个公开驾驶数据集，统一定义16类横向与6类纵向动作；采用多源标注系统融合车辆信号、自车位姿、高精地图拓扑与文本描述等证据分配标签；标准化时间动作边界，并通过运动学一致性检查与人工复核验证。另提出DenseEgo视频基线，将空间感知表征与稠密逐步动作监督相结合，并提供长尾感知评估协议。

Result: 基准包含86.6K片段、190.8小时、覆盖26个国家；DenseEgo取得横向82.7、纵向83.5 mAP，分别超出最强现有视频模型1.5与5.0个点；并示范了与语言模型智能体结合进行动作与场景检索。

Conclusion: EgoManeuver提供了统一、细粒度且长尾感知的自车动作识别基准，DenseEgo验证了稠密监督的有效性，并为基于语言智能体的驾驶视频检索奠定基础。

Abstract: Fine-grained ego-vehicle action recognition turns large-scale driving video into structured, searchable driving scenarios, providing a foundation for discovering critical maneuvers and systematically developing and evaluating autonomous driving models. However, large-scale driving videos often lack explicit ego-action labels, and existing datasets vary in action definitions, annotation granularity, and available supervision. We introduce EgoManeuver, a unified benchmark of 16 lateral and 6 longitudinal actions constructed from seven public driving datasets. Our multi-source labeling system leverages existing annotations and complementary evidence available in each source dataset, including vehicle signals, ego poses, HD-map topology, and captions, to assign labels under a unified fine-grained action taxonomy. We further standardize temporal action boundaries across datasets and validate the resulting labels through kinematic consistency checks and targeted human review. The resulting benchmark contains 86.6K segments spanning 190.8 h across 26 countries and provides a long-tail-aware evaluation protocol. We also introduce DenseEgo, a video-based baseline that combines spatially aware representations with dense per-step action supervision. On the EgoManeuver, DenseEgo achieves 82.7 lateral and 83.5 longitudinal mAP, outperforming the strongest existing video model by 1.5 and 5.0 points, respectively. Finally, we demonstrate one way to integrate DenseEgo with a language-model agent for retrieving driving videos that match user-specified actions and scenarios. All code, datasets, and model weights will be publicly released.

</details>


### [54] [Recall4D: Forgetting, Not Misalignment, Limits Long-Horizon Feed-Forward 4D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33655)
*Tianle Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出训练无关框架Recall4D，通过持久世界状态与稀疏运动锚点解决前馈4D追踪器在长视频窗口串联中的"遗忘"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈4D追踪器在短片段上表现良好，但长视频中窗口串联会显著降低精度。作者发现误差来源并非窗口间对齐失败，而是"遗忘"：对已离开视野的静态点重复从不再观测它的窗口进行预测，会破坏此前恢复的几何，且误差随后续观测时间间隔增长。

Method: 冻结主干追踪器，仅做外部增强：(1) 持久世界状态——视野外的静态点保留其世界坐标，视野内则由稀疏关键帧解码的观测取平均进行精修；(2) 动态物体——追踪器仅解码稀疏代表性锚点，再用几何蒙皮(skinning)将锚点位移传播到稠密查询点。另发现仅保留未见点的最后观测位置即可在PointOdyssey上降误差26%。

Result: 在两个长时程基准上，Recall4D在提升主干精度的同时实现7.6–9.0倍加速；精度增益可迁移至另外五个主干和三个长序列任务，显示良好泛化性。

Conclusion: 长视频4D追踪的核心瓶颈是状态记忆而非窗口对齐，显式持久记忆加稀疏锚点传播是高效且通用的解决路径。

Abstract: Feed-forward 4D trackers recover camera motion, depth, and point trajectories from short video clips, but chaining windows over long videos degrades their accuracy. We find that replacing inter-window alignment with ground-truth transforms leaves tracking error essentially unchanged. Static-point error instead grows with time since last observation: repeatedly predicting a static point from windows that no longer observe it corrupts previously recovered geometry. We call this failure forgetting. Merely retaining the last observed positions of unseen points reduces error by 26% on PointOdyssey without modifying the tracker. We introduce Recall4D, a training-free framework that augments a frozen tracker with a persistent world state and sparse motion anchors. Static points retain their world-space positions outside the camera view and are refined by averaging observations decoded at sparse keyframes while in view. For dynamic objects, the tracker decodes sparse representative anchors, while geometric skinning propagates their displacements to dense queries. Across two long-horizon benchmarks, Recall4D improves the accuracy of its backbone while running 7.6-9.0$\times$ faster, and its accuracy gains carry over to five further backbones and three other long-sequence tasks, demonstrating its generality.

</details>


### [55] [Look at What Matters: Learning Lesion Prototypes from Severity Labels for Domain-Generalized Diabetic Retinopathy Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33671)
*Ziyang Zhang,Juming Xiong,Yanfan Zhu,Yuechen Yang,Junchao Zhu,Zhengyi Lu,Chongyu Qu,Yiming Zhong,Yalin Zheng,Yuankai Huo,He Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以病变原型证据构建DR分级决策的多实例原型学习框架，跨六个数据集留一域平均QWK达0.761。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DR分级深度模型在未见影像域上泛化差，原因是预测可能依赖源数据集的特定相关性（捷径特征）而非视网膜病变本身。

Method: 仅用图像严重度标签，将DR临床分级含义转化为多实例约束，在多尺度上竞争性学习病变与背景原型；采用加性序数评分模型，仅由单个病变原型响应预测严重度，不设全局特征分类通路；引入教师引导的退化保持与针对持久坐标偏好的空间正则化。

Result: 六个公开DR数据集留一域协议下平均二次加权kappa为0.761，较最强对比方法高0.036；定位与原型移除分析表明响应与视网膜病变对齐并对分级有贡献；五种其他原型架构及图像退化实验验证了通用性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 以病变证据驱动的可解释原型框架能提升DR分级跨域泛化与鲁棒性，并使决策证据可验证。

Abstract: Deep learning models for diabetic retinopathy (DR) grading often generalize poorly to unseen imaging domains, in part because predictions may exploit correlations specific to source datasets rather than retinal pathology. We address this challenge by constructing grading decisions from lesion evidence learned using only image severity labels. Clinical implications of DR grades provide multiple instance constraints that guide competitive learning of lesion and background prototypes at multiple scales. An additive ordinal scoring model then predicts severity exclusively from individual lesion prototype responses, explicitly connecting learned evidence with grading decisions without a global feature classification pathway. To improve the reliability of this evidence, we introduce teacher guided preservation under image degradations and spatial regularization against persistent coordinate preferences. Across six public DR datasets under a leave one domain out protocol, our method attains a mean quadratic weighted kappa of 0.761, outperforming the strongest competing method by 0.036. Localization and prototype removal analyses show that the learned responses align with retinal lesions and contribute to grading decisions. Experiments across five other prototype architectures and under image degradations further demonstrate the generality and robustness of the approach.

</details>


### [56] [MOVR-SAR: A Multi-resolution SAR Registration Dataset under Orbit-crossing and View-variant Imaging Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33673)
*Yuchi Pu,Bosong Qi,Pengcheng Shi,Jiayuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MOVR-SAR——一个大规模、几何感知的多分辨率SAR图像配准数据集（450,845对配准并质量筛选的瓦片对，源自Capella、Umbra、Sentinel-1、NISAR四星座2774个场景对），覆盖X/C/L波段与0.25–20 m GSD，并建立基于轨道方向与视向的四类成像几何对分类及视角间隔标注框架，系统评测并验证了微调对跨几何配准性能的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同获取几何（轨道方向、侧视方向、入射角）会改变后向散射、引入地形畸变并影响特征可见性，使SAR-SAR配准极具挑战；但现有大规模、几何感知的基准数据严重匮乏，导致模型在跨几何条件下性能不明、偏差难以量化。

Method: 构建MOVR-SAR多分辨率数据集：从四星座2774个场景对生成450,845个配准且经质量筛选的瓦片对，覆盖X/C/L波段与0.25–20 m GSD，涵盖多样区域与地类；提出统一框架，依据轨道方向与侧视方向定义四类成像几何对，并为Capella与Umbra提供视角间隔标签，实现同向/反向照射的分层评测；对代表性匹配方法在官方权重、跨模态预训练权重及MOVR-SAR微调三种设置下进行系统评估。

Result: 官方与跨模态预训练权重在高分辨率X波段影像上表现较差；在MOVR-SAR上微调后精度大幅提升并缩小跨平台、跨几何对类别与跨视角间隔的差异：FlexGlue AUC@5在Capella上由3.06%升至89.79%，Umbra上由1.79%升至89.03%，Sentinel-1与NISAR分别达93.41%与94.77%。微调显著削弱官方权重下"同轨道同侧视"的固有优势，并在Capella上反转三方法平均类别排序。地类效应仍呈平台与方法依赖性。

Conclusion: 跨几何配准中的性能偏差主要源于训练数据覆盖不足，而非跨几何匹配的固有局限；MOVR-SAR为稳健的SAR-SAR配准提供了训练资源与几何感知基准，数据集与模型权重将于GitHub开源。

Abstract: Synthetic aperture radar (SAR) image registration under varying acquisition geometries is challenging because orbit direction, look side, and incidence angle affect backscatter, terrain-induced distortion, and feature visibility. However, large-scale geometry-aware benchmarks remain scarce. This paper presents MOVR-SAR, a multi-resolution dataset of 450,845 registered and quality-screened tile pairs derived from 2774 scene pairs from Capella, Umbra, Sentinel-1, and NISAR. It covers X, C, and L bands, ground sampling distances of 0.25-20 m, and diverse regions and land-cover types. A unified framework defines four imaging-geometry pair categories from orbit direction and look side, plus viewing-angle-separation labels for Capella and Umbra, enabling stratified evaluation under same- and opposite-direction illumination. Systematic evaluation of representative matching methods shows that official and cross-modal pretrained weights perform poorly on high-resolution X-band imagery. Fine-tuning on MOVR-SAR improves accuracy and reduces disparities across platforms, imaging-geometry pair categories, and viewing-angle separations. For example, fine-tuning increases FlexGlue AUC@5 from 3.06% to 89.79% on Capella and from 1.79% to 89.03% on Umbra, reaching 93.41% and 94.77% on Sentinel-1 and NISAR, respectively. Fine-tuning substantially reduces the official-weight same-orbit, same-look-side advantage and reverses the three-method average category ranking on Capella, suggesting that this bias reflects training-data coverage rather than an invariant limitation of cross-geometry matching. Land-cover effects remain platform- and method-dependent. MOVR-SAR provides a training resource and geometry-aware benchmark for robust SAR-SAR registration. The dataset and model weights will be released at https://github.com/fdhzhz5870/MOVR-SAR.

</details>


### [57] [WorldUAV: Scaling UAV Data Synthesis via World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33682)
*Xuewen Liu,Mengjuan Chen,Jing Zhang,Mengzhe Shen,Zekun Xu,Zibo Chen,Zuoxiu Zhang,Yue Dong,Zhikai Li,Qingyi Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WorldUAV：基于世界模型的自动化框架，将无人机直接合成进真实视频，构建包含6,878段视频和205,778张图像的高质量无人机数据集，显著提升检测性能并将单图构造成本降至15秒与0.0005美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机检测与跟踪受限于高质量训练数据稀缺：游戏引擎合成数据存在显著合成-真实域差距，而真实数据人工采集成本高、规模受限。

Method: 以世界模型为核心构建高效数据生成框架，将UAV直接嵌入现成真实视频中，保留真实背景、光照与场景动态；利用世界模型的时空先验生成具备合理运动、姿态变化与跨帧一致性的视频。进一步设计由多模态大语言模型编排的智能体流水线，自动完成源数据获取、轨迹规划、合成调用与视频校验，实现全自动数据集构建。

Result: 构建WorldUAV数据集，含6,878段UAV视频与205,778张UAV图像；实验表明其显著提升UAV检测器性能；每张高质量UAV图像仅需15秒与0.0005美元API成本，大幅降低构造成本。

Conclusion: WorldUAV同时提供了高质量数据集与可扩展的数据生成范式，为无人机检测与跟踪的数据瓶颈提供低成本、自动化的解决路径。

Abstract: UAV detection and tracking remain constrained by the scarcity of high-quality training data. Existing game-engine-based synthetic UAV data suffer from a substantial synthetic-to-real gap, while the high cost of manually collected real-world UAV data limits dataset scale. Therefore, we introduce WorldUAV, a high-quality UAV dataset constructed through an efficient framework powered by world models. Specifically, WorldUAV directly integrates UAVs into readily available real-world videos, naturally preserving realistic backgrounds, illumination, and scene dynamics. Leveraging the spatiotemporal priors of world models, it generates UAV videos with plausible motion, pose variations, and cross-frame consistency, effectively narrowing the gap between synthetic and real data. We further develop an agentic pipeline orchestrated by a multimodal large language model that acquires sources, plans UAV trajectories, invokes synthesis, and verifies generated videos, enabling fully automated dataset construction. WorldUAV comprises 6,878 UAV videos and 205,778 UAV images. Experiments show that WorldUAV significantly improves the performance of UAV detectors. Moreover, it substantially reduces dataset construction costs, requiring only 15 seconds and USD 0.0005 API cost per high-quality UAV image. Our work offers both a high-quality dataset and a scalable data generation paradigm for UAV detection and tracking.

</details>


### [58] [Read Before You Use: Improving Visual Memory Utilization in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33687)
*Junseok Kim,Kyungmin Min,Kyomin Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出现有视觉语言模型即使获得充足的多模态记忆证据仍难以回答下游问题，将其归因于"证据读取"与"下游问答"两个环节的失败，并提出两阶段训练框架MemRU（Read SFT + Use GRPO）加以改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态记忆使长程视觉语言智能体能够保存和检索过往经验，但已有工作多聚焦记忆的构建与检索，如何有效"使用"已提供的记忆仍是难题。实验观察显示，即使给出充分的多模态证据，VLM仍常无法回答下游问题；将记忆图像替换为参考字幕可带来显著性能提升，说明模型在直接读取视觉证据方面存在困难。然而模型生成的字幕可能遗漏关键信息，故该替代方案在实际中受限，因而有必要深入剖析记忆利用失败的具体环节。

Method: 作者将记忆利用失败区分为"证据读取"与"下游问答"两类，并据此提出两阶段训练框架MemRU：第一阶段通过监督微调（Read SFT）提升模型读取视觉证据的能力；第二阶段采用组相对策略优化（Group Relative Policy Optimization, GRPO）强化下游任务中对证据的利用。

Result: 实验结果（摘要以定性描述为主）表明，该8B模型可泛化至三个未见过的多模态记忆系统，性能优于Qwen3-VL-32B，并接近GPT-5-mini。

Conclusion: 记忆利用问题应拆分为证据读取与下游证据使用两个可分别优化的子问题；分阶段训练（SFT+GRPO）能显著提升VLM对多模态记忆的利用效率与跨系统泛化能力，小规模模型亦可取得具有竞争力的表现。

Abstract: Multimodal memory enables long-horizon vision-language agents to preserve and retrieve past experiences. While prior research has focused on memory construction and retrieval, effectively using the provided memory remains a challenge for vision-language models (VLMs). We find that VLMs often fail to answer downstream questions even when sufficient multimodal evidence is provided. To investigate why VLMs struggle, we replace memory images with reference captions and observe substantial performance gains, suggesting difficulties in reading visual evidence directly. However, model-generated captions may omit critical information, limiting this approach in practice. These limitations motivate a closer examination of memory utilization, revealing failures in both evidence reading and downstream question answering. Building on this distinction, we propose \textbf{MemRU}, a two-stage training framework that improves evidence reading through supervised fine-tuning (Read SFT) and downstream evidence utilization through Group Relative Policy Optimization (Use GRPO). Experimental results demonstrate that our 8B model generalizes across three unseen multimodal memory systems, outperforming Qwen3-VL-32B and approaching GPT-5-mini.

</details>


### [59] [GeoTex: Geometry-as-Texture Encoding and History-Grounded 4D Occupancy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33693)
*Shijun Guo,Yaming Yang,Haoran Xu,Wei Zhao,Ziyu Guan,Cai Xu,Peixi Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoTex 是一个占用世界模型，用"几何即纹理"编码和"以历史为锚"的预测策略实现 4D 占用预测，相对 GenieDrive 平均预测 mIoU 提升 9.79%、IoU 提升 11.91%，参数从 3.47M 降至 2.33M，且长时域增益更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶世界模型需预测 3D 场景演化。语义占用将颜色纹理抽象为离散标签，局部形状辨识几乎仅依赖 3D 体素排布，故几何线索至关重要。现有方法直接借用图像编码框架，通过高度通道化或平面分解获取紧凑隐表示，但在压缩前未充分联合建模列内高度模式与列间关系，削弱了几何线索；预测端也未能充分利用互补的历史信息，且难以在滚动推演中保留可靠的观测证据。

Method: GeoTex 表示：在压缩前联合编码列内高度模式与列间关系，形成紧凑连续隐表示，并通过内容相关的高度展开与几何交互重建 3D 结构。GeoTex 预测：以固定跨观测关系记忆在整段 rollout 中保留初始证据，并通过目标参照的残差更新引入互补的近期历史。预测训练完成后，采用 decoder-only 适配提升对预测状态的语义解码能力。

Result: 相比 GenieDrive，平均预测 mIoU 提升 9.79%、IoU 提升 11.91%，参数量由 3.47M 降至 2.33M；预测时域越长，增益越明显。代码见补充材料。

Conclusion: 联合建模列内/列间几何并配合历史锚定的预测与轻量解码适配，可在更小参数下显著提升 4D 占用预测精度，且优势随预测时域延长而扩大。

Abstract: 4D occupancy forecasting models 3D scene evolution to support future prediction and scenario generation in autonomous-driving world models. Semantic occupancy abstracts image color and texture into discrete labels, leaving 3D voxel arrangements as the primary cues for distinguishing local shapes within each class. Existing methods borrow image-encoding frameworks to obtain compact latent representations through height channelization or plane factorization, but do not sufficiently model within-column height patterns and cross-column relations jointly before compression, weakening these geometric cues. Forecasting also underutilizes complementary history and struggles to retain reliable observation evidence. We therefore propose GeoTex, an occupancy world model combining geometry-as-texture encoding with history-grounded forecasting. GeoTex Representation jointly encodes within-column height patterns and cross-column relations into compact continuous latent representations and restores 3D structure through content-dependent height unfolding and geometric interaction. GeoTex Prediction retains initial evidence throughout rollout in fixed cross-observation relational memory and incorporates complementary recent history through target-referenced residual updates. After prediction training, decoder-only adaptation improves semantic decoding of predicted states. GeoTex improves average forecasting mIoU and IoU by 9.79% and 11.91% relative to GenieDrive while reducing parameters from 3.47M to 2.33M, with gains increasing at longer horizons. Code is included in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [60] [Efficient Scene-Distribution-Aware Second-Order Optimization for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33698)
*Hyunseung Son,Young Sun Choi,Youngsik Yun,Kibeom Hong,Youngjung Uh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多视图二阶3DGS优化方法：用考虑视图重叠的视图选择策略减少冗余，并用联合多视图像素处理与复用的透射率/累积颜色缓存，将采样二阶基线的优化时间缩短一半以上并获得更高PSNR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS逐视图更新需数万次迭代；多视图批处理可降低对单一训练视图的依赖，二阶优化能以更大、结构化的参数更新减少迭代次数。但迭代次数少意味着纠正"仅拟合单次更新所用视图"的机会减少，因此每次二阶更新必须覆盖更充分的场景；而多视图二阶更新计算开销大，像素采样虽可降本，仍存在视图冗余与逐视图重复计算alpha合成的问题。

Method: 1) 视图选择策略：依据视图间重叠程度挑选视图，减少冗余视图；2) 两项实现策略：将多视图的采样像素放入联合通道统一处理，并在渲染过程中缓存并复用透射率与累积颜色，避免从头重算alpha合成。

Result: 在三个数据集上，本方法PSNR高于采样的二阶基线，优化时间不到其一半，SSIM与LPIPS具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过在视图选择层面抑制冗余、在实现层面合并多视图采样像素处理并复用渲染中间量，多视图二阶3DGS优化可在显著缩短训练时间的同时提升重建质量，说明"覆盖率+计算复用"是可行且有效的折中方向。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) optimization updates the Gaussian parameters one training view at a time, tens of thousands of times. Unlike such single-view updates, multi-view batches reduce dependence on individual training views, and second-order optimization reduces the number of updates with larger, structured parameter changes. Because fewer updates leave fewer chances to correct a change that fits only the views used in one update, scene coverage becomes more important for each second-order update. Multi-view second-order updates, however, are expensive. Pixel sampling reduces this cost, but two issues remain. First, selecting views without considering overlap leaves some views redundant. To address this, we propose a view selection strategy that reduces this redundancy. Second, existing work processes the sampled pixels of each view in a separate pass and recomputes alpha compositing for these pixels from scratch. We introduce two implementation strategies that reduce both overheads by processing the sampled pixels of multiple views in joint passes and reusing the transmittance and accumulated color cached during rendering. On three datasets, our method reaches higher PSNR than the sampled second-order baseline in less than half its optimization time, with competitive SSIM and LPIPS.

</details>


### [61] [Nepali Currency Denomination Classification Using Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and MobileNetV2: An Experimental Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33713)
*Sakar Lamichhane,Sandesh Bhatta,Aayush Adhikari,Aaron Sapkota*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文构建含16,249张图像的尼泊尔卢比数据集，比较常规CNN、MobileNetV2迁移学习与MobileNetV2超参数调优三种配置识别7类面额；调优模型验证准确率最高(95%)但测试仅84%，未调优迁移学习测试89%，并基于TensorFlow Lite与Flask搭建支持上传与摄像头识别、含语音输出的Web原型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动货币面额识别在现金处理、金融科技与辅助系统中具有应用价值，需评估轻量级深度学习方案在尼泊尔卢比上的可行性与部署能力。

Method: 7类面额(5/10/20/50/100/500/1000卢比)；数据划分为训练10,500、验证3,500、独立测试2,249张；图像统一缩放至128×128并归一化，施加方向、尺度、光照与外观增强；模型为常规CNN、MobileNetV2迁移学习、MobileNetV2超参数调优；评价指标包括准确率、精确率、召回率、F1及PR曲线与ROC分析；部署采用Flask + TensorFlow Lite，支持图片上传与实时摄像头识别，提供视觉与语音输出。

Result: 调优MobileNetV2验证准确率最高(95%)，精确率/召回率/F1为0.93/0.91/0.92，但测试集仅84%；未调优迁移学习测试准确率89%；常规CNN为85%。调优带来的验证精度提升未转化为测试精度提升。

Conclusion: 在所评估条件下迁移学习是具竞争力的方案；轻量级深度学习结合语音辅助Web界面具备实际可行性；未来需扩充数据多样性、提升非受控成像条件下的鲁棒性并开展更广泛的真实场景评测。

Abstract: Automated currency denomination recognition is a computer vision task with applications in cash handling, financial technology, and assistive systems. This study classifies seven Nepali currency denominations (Rs. 5, 10, 20, 50, 100, 500, and 1,000) using three configurations: a conventional convolutional neural network (CNN), MobileNetV2 with transfer learning, and MobileNetV2 with hyperparameter tuning. A compiled dataset of 16,249 images was used, comprising 10,500 training, 3,500 validation, and 2,249 independent test images. Images were resized to 128 x 128 pixels, normalized, and augmented to vary orientation, scale, illumination, and appearance. Models were evaluated using accuracy, precision, recall, F1-score, and precision-recall and ROC analysis. The hyperparameter-tuned MobileNetV2 achieved the highest validation accuracy (95\%), with precision, recall, and F1-score of 0.93, 0.91, and 0.92, but reached only 84\% accuracy on the held-out test set. MobileNetV2 with transfer learning but no additional tuning achieved 89\% test accuracy, and the conventional CNN achieved 85\%. Transfer learning therefore provided a competitive approach under the evaluated conditions, while higher validation accuracy from tuning did not yield higher test accuracy. To demonstrate practical deployment, a Flask-based web prototype using TensorFlow Lite was developed, supporting image upload and live webcam recognition with both visual and voice output. The study shows the feasibility of combining lightweight deep learning with a voice-assisted web interface for Nepali currency recognition, and highlights the need for more diverse datasets, greater robustness under uncontrolled imaging conditions, and broader real-world evaluation.

</details>


### [62] [VTK-Bench: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Distinguishing Language Priors and Text Modal Bias in Visual Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33725)
*Yao Lu,Yuhang Zheng,Yaxin Gao,Zhe Tang,Zhaowei Zhu,Jiaheng Wei,Yunhao Wang,Shanqing Yu,Qi Xuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VTK-Bench基准，将视觉、文本与先验知识三源信息显式分离，用于细粒度归因VLM幻觉成因，发现语言先验比文本模态偏置更普遍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉基准多停留在识别幻觉及描述表现，忽视其成因；多模态幻觉可源于文本模态偏置（TMB）与语言先验（LP）两种不同失效模式，需要可区分二者的评测手段。

Method: 构建VTK-Bench，显式分离视觉信息（V）、文本信息（T）与先验知识（K）三类信息源，为V/T/K设置互斥答案，从而对模型回答进行细粒度归因；引入离散归因与软归因两类指标刻画信息源偏好。在11个代表性开源VLM上开展实验。

Result: 多数受测模型中LP比TMB更为普遍；TMB随模型规模增大显著下降，而LP改善相对有限，仍是主导性失效模式。

Conclusion: 区分信息源偏好对深入理解多模态幻觉至关重要；模型扩展主要缓解文本模态偏置而非语言先验，提示需针对性设计缓解策略。

Abstract: Visual language models (VLMs) have demonstrated exceptional capabilities across various multimodal tasks, but they remain susceptible to hallucinations, undermining their reliability. Existing hallucination benchmarks primarily focus on identifying hallucinations and describing their manifestations, often neglecting their underlying causes. Specifically, multimodal hallucinations can stem from two distinct failure modes: text modality bias (TMB), reflecting an over-reliance on misleading text input; and language prior (LP), reflecting a reliance on previously learned knowledge. To differentiate these failure modes, we propose VTK-Bench, a benchmark that explicitly separates three information sources: visual information (V), textual information (T), and prior knowledge (K). By establishing mutually exclusive answers corresponding to V, T, and K, VTK-Bench enables fine-grained attribution analysis of model responses. Furthermore, we introduce discrete attribution and soft attribution metrics to characterize information source preferences. Extensive experiments on 11 representative open-source VLMs show that LP is more prevalent than TMB in most evaluated models. Notably, TMB decreases substantially with model scaling, whereas LP exhibits relatively limited improvement and remains the dominant failure mode. These findings highlight the importance of different information source preferences for a deeper understanding of multimodal hallucinations.

</details>


### [63] [DualCast: Dual-Image Conditioned Diffusion Transformers for Digital Human E-Commerce Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33734)
*Lifan Jiang,Yifan Huang,Jixuan Zhang,Peifeng Wang,Yuying Ma,Dehai Zhao,Deng Cai,Boxi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GameWeaver：统一扩散框架，通过相机位姿估计、指定轨迹视频生成、参考图+文本联合生成三项互补任务，自动产出视频–相机配对数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏交互与控制的世界模型需要大规模"游戏视频+相机轨迹"配对数据，但同步采集相机轨迹依赖游戏专用管线，难以规模化。

Method: 提出 tipxel 表示：每像素存储其射线端点在参考坐标系下的归一化3D坐标，得到三通道 tipxel 场，与RGB帧共享预训练视频模型的VAE；采用全可训练双流Transformer，通过双向门控注意力交换外观与几何特征，并以共享文本特征对两流进行条件化；最后由确定性拟合从生成的 tipxel 场恢复相机内参与归一化轨迹。

Result: 相机控制任务归一化平移MSE为0.0029；联合生成任务指令遵循度85.48/100，优于最佳级联方案的64.71。

Conclusion: 验证了以统一扩散框架自动生成配对视频–相机数据的可行性，可替代游戏专用采集管线。

Abstract: Recent advances in world models for game interaction and control have increased the demand for large-scale gameplay videos paired with camera trajectories. However, capturing synchronized camera trajectories often requires game-specific pipelines, limiting scalability. We therefore propose GameWeaver, a unified diffusion framework that automatically produces paired video--camera data through three complementary tasks: camera pose estimation from observed video, video generation along a prescribed camera trajectory, and joint video--camera generation from a reference image and text. First, each tipxel stores the normalized 3D coordinates of a pixel ray's endpoint in a reference coordinate frame. The resulting three-channel tipxel fields and RGB frames share the pretrained video model's VAE. Next, a fully trainable dual-stream Transformer exchanges appearance and geometry features through bidirectional gated attention and conditions both streams on shared text features. Finally, deterministic fitting recovers camera intrinsics and normalized trajectories from generated tipxel fields. In our evaluation tasks, GameWeaver achieves a normalized translation MSE of 0.0029 for camera control; for joint generation, it scores 85.48 out of 100 on instruction adherence versus 64.71 for the best cascade on that metric, validating the feasibility of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [64] [Teaching Students to Stand on Their Own: Distilling Vision Foundation Models for Fine-Grained Few-Shot Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33749)
*Junsu Kim,Seungryul Baek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STAND：面向细粒度少样本类增量学习的VFM到学生模型系统性蒸馏框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VFM直接作主干计算成本高且不保证细粒度识别能力；知识蒸馏虽降低推理成本，但初步实验发现蒸馏学生模型在新类上仍显著落后于VFM教师。

Method: 提出STAND：固定复制教师分类器给学生；用投影器对齐二者输出；输入由不同训练图像合成的复合图像。在复合图像上进行关系蒸馏（对齐教师与学生刻画每张图像与其他图像的关系方式）和差异蒸馏（对齐该关系在两幅复合图像间的变化）。最后冻结学生模型，使用长度校准原型对新类分类，以恢复少样本原型被削弱的相似度。

Result: 在FG-FSCIL数据集上以低计算成本取得SOTA，并可跨学生主干与更广泛数据集泛化。

Conclusion: 系统性VFM-to-student蒸馏设计能有效缩小师生差距，兼顾细粒度识别性能与推理效率。

Abstract: Fine-grained few-shot class-incremental learning (FG-FSCIL) aims to incrementally learn visually similar new classes from limited images without catastrophic forgetting. Prior work typically learns strong representations on base classes or leverages vision foundation models (VFMs) directly as backbones or via knowledge distillation. However, VFM backbones incur high computational costs and do not guarantee fine-grained recognition. Although knowledge distillation reduces inference cost, our preliminary experiments reveal that distilled students still lag substantially behind their VFM teachers, particularly on new classes. To address this limitation, we propose STAND, a systematic VFM-to-student distillation design tailored for FG-FSCIL. Specifically, STAND connects the teacher and student by equipping the student with a fixed copy of the teacher's classifier, aligning their outputs via projectors, and feeding both models composite images that combine different training images. On these composite images, relation distillation matches how the teacher and the student relate each image to other images, while difference distillation matches how this relation changes between two composite images. Finally, we freeze the student and classify new classes using length-calibrated prototypes, which restore the reduced similarity of few-shot prototypes. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that STAND achieves state-of-the-art performance across FG-FSCIL datasets at low computational cost, while generalizing across student backbones and broader datasets.

</details>


### [65] [Spectral Colorimetry Based on GMoE for Temperature Measurement in Explosion Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33750)
*Jie Fang,Wenhao Li,Jianning Yang,Nan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出门控专家混合（GMoE）框架，结合物理先验与深度学习分而治之策略，实现爆炸场光谱测温，平均RMSE 12.70±1.32 K、MAE 7.96±0.86 K，较传统光谱比色法分别降低63.4%和70.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爆炸场光谱测温可避免接触式方法干扰温度分布并适应高温场景，但物理模型驱动方法依赖单一辐射亮度比回归，难以适应宽温区（灰度比与辐射亮度比映射随温度显著变化）；数据驱动方法受训练数据稀缺制约，爆炸场敏感性加剧该问题。

Method: GMoE框架：将任务分解为多个专家子任务，每个专家用物理标定的多项式函数建模特定谱段对的灰度比—辐射亮度比关系，再由含相应参数的物理模型估计温度；设计门控网络自适应加权各专家输出并生成最终温度估计，实验中采用Transformer作为门控网络。

Result: Transformer门控的GMoE取得最佳性能，平均RMSE 12.70±1.32 K，平均MAE 7.96±0.86 K；相比传统光谱比色法，平均RMSE与MAE分别降低63.4%与70.8%。

Conclusion: 物理先验与分而治之深度学习相结合可有效缓解宽温区适应性与数据稀缺问题，实现高精度爆炸场光谱测温，优于传统光谱比色法。

Abstract: Spectroscopic thermometry in explosion fields has attracted considerable attention in related engineering domains, primarily because it can mitigate the interference of traditional contact methods with temperature distributions and address the limitations of high-temperature measurement scenarios. However, existing physics-model-driven methods typically rely on regressing a single radiance-ratio pair, which limits their adaptability to wide-range temperature estimation. This limitation arises from notable variations in the mapping between grayscale ratios and radiance ratios at different temperatures. In contrast, existing data-driven methods often suffer from limited performance due to the scarcity of training data, a challenge exacerbated by the sensitivity of explosion fields. In this work, we propose a gated mixture of experts (GMoE) framework that integrates a divide-and-conquer strategy in deep learning with physical prior knowledge for temperature estimation, thereby decomposing the complex task into multiple expert-specific subtasks. Specifically, each expert is developed using physically calibrated polynomial functions to model the relationship between grayscale ratios and radiance ratios for a given pair of spectral bands, with temperature then estimated using a physical model incorporating the corresponding parameters. Additionally, a gating network is designed to adaptively weight the outputs of different experts and generate the final temperature estimate. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves the best performance when implemented with the Transformer-based gating network, yielding a mean RMSE of $12.70 \pm 1.32$ K and a mean MAE of $7.96 \pm 0.86$ K. Compared with conventional spectral colorimetry, the proposed framework reduces the mean RMSE and MAE by 63.4\% and 70.8\%, respectively.

</details>


### [66] [VAPOR: Improving Multimodal Reasoning with Sparse Visual Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33751)
*Mingyu Su,Wei Wu,Erle Zhu,Chengxing Xie,Deyao Hong,Hongning Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VAPOR 是一个免训练框架，通过复用并选择性刷新视觉注意力输出，降低多模态大模型长视觉上下文解码的计算冗余，实现1.11–1.21倍加速并提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归解码时每个生成token都重算视觉注意力，即使相邻token依赖相似的视觉证据，造成大量冗余计算；同时需避免长推理中的视觉接地漂移。

Method: 缓存视觉注意力输出、每步仍更新文本注意力；用低秩近似估计视觉注意力质量，结合prefill注意力一致性与缓存年龄，为每个注意力头独立做出Persist/Refresh决策；保留完整视觉KV缓存以保证可按需访问全部视觉token。

Result: 在两类MLLM家族、8个图像和4个视频推理基准上，几乎全部基准精度优于稠密注意力，解码加速1.11–1.21倍；相比最强SOTA加速基线，精度再提升0.35–2.15个百分点，延迟相当或更低。

Conclusion: 按需复用视觉注意力是兼顾效率与精度的有效免训练加速途径，逐头决策与完整KV保留可缓解漂移并保持性能。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made substantial progress in visual reasoning, but extended generation over large visual contexts remains computationally expensive. During autoregressive decoding, visual attention is recomputed for every generated token, even when consecutive tokens rely on similar visual evidence. We introduce VAPOR (Visual Attention Persistence with On-demand Refresh), a training-free framework that reduces this redundancy by reusing visual attention outputs across decoding steps and refreshing them when needed. VAPOR caches the visual attention output while continuing to update textual attention at every step, helping reduce visual grounding drift during extended reasoning. To decide when to reuse cached outputs, VAPOR estimates visual attention mass using a low-rank approximation. It also uses prefill-based attention consistency and cache age to make independent Persist and Refresh decisions for each attention head. VAPOR retains the full visual key-value (KV) cache, so all visual tokens remain available when new evidence is needed. Experiments on eight image and four video reasoning benchmarks across two MLLM families show that VAPOR improves accuracy over dense attention on nearly all benchmarks while achieving 1.11-1.21x decoding speedups. Compared with the strongest state-of-the-art acceleration baseline in each setting, VAPOR further improves accuracy by 0.35-2.15 percentage points while achieving comparable or lower decoding latency. Our code is available at https://github.com/Su-my/VAPOR.

</details>


### [67] [Show Me the Change You Want: Visual Example-Guided Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33756)
*Yuan Qian,Jiaqi Cui,Jie Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出视觉示例引导的变化检测（VECD）：用一对未标注的前后图像作为"示例"来指定用户想要的某类变化，并在另一对图像中定位该类变化，无需文本、示例掩码或测试时微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指代式变化检测依赖预定义类别、文本描述或标注掩码，灵活性差且需额外标注。用户实际只关心某一特定类型的变化，因此希望以直观的图像示例来表达需求。

Method: 从单张图像学习可比较的属性状态表示，并引入以示例为条件的读出模块（example-conditioned readout），在不同物体与场景间匹配变化模式；示例本身定义任务，不使用文本、示例掩码或测试时微调。

Result: 在EditCLEVR、MagicBrush、HumanEdit、MaskEdit四个数据集上评估：单一固定示例对可跨场景、跨数据集引导检测且无需微调；在多变化并存场景中能选出匹配的变化，请求变化缺失时返回空掩码；对配对训练中未出现的属性族仍有泛化能力，四种留出设置下mIoU为74.54%–93.38%。

Conclusion: 以视觉示例作为查询条件的变化检测范式可行，兼具灵活性、零标注负担与强跨域泛化能力，为可控变化检测提供了新方向。

Abstract: Change detection identifies where images differ, but in practice users often seek only a particular kind of change. Existing referring change detection methods rely on predefined categories, textual descriptions, or annotated masks, limiting flexibility or requiring additional annotation. In this paper, we propose a new way to perform change detection, where an unannotated before-and-after image pair serves as a visual example to guide change localization in another pair. Our framework, Visual Example-Guided Change Detection (VECD), learns comparable attribute states from single images and introduces an example-conditioned readout to match changes across different objects and scenes. The example itself defines the request, without text, example masks, or test-time fine-tuning. We evaluate VECD on four datasets spanning rendered scenes and natural image edits: EditCLEVR, MagicBrush, HumanEdit, and MaskEdit. Experiments demonstrate cross-dataset generalization without fine-tuning: a single fixed example pair guides change detection across multiple scenes, even when examples and queries originate from different datasets. VECD selects matching changes in scenes with multiple simultaneous changes and returns an empty mask when the requested change is absent. It further generalizes to attribute families withheld from paired training, achieving 74.54\% to 93.38\% mIoU across four holdout settings. Code is available at https://github.com/qianyuancs/VECD

</details>


### [68] [GlassGround: GUI Grounding Beyond Native Screenshots with Egocentric Wearable Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33760)
*Yun Wang,Siyue Sun,Jiaying Du,Xinyi Wei,Xiaoming Zhai,Zhenlin An,Ninghao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GlassGround基准（3,250任务，Android/iOS，4种穿戴设备×4种视角），揭示GUI grounding从截图94.8%跌至相机图像48.5%；分析表明目标尺度、屏幕几何与背景变化不足以解释差距；提出PairDistill跨视图表征对齐基线，相机图像精度提升9.4/12.2点并可跨设备跨OS迁移，配合局部放大达69.6%/64.4%，但差距仍未弥合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴助手在无法直接获取截图时必须从相机观测中定位界面元素，而现有GUI grounding基准几乎只评估原生截图，缺乏对穿戴相机视角下性能退化的系统刻画。

Method: 构建配对视图基准：固定GUI状态、指令与目标，仅改变观测模态（原生截图 vs 穿戴相机图像）；覆盖Android与iOS、4款穿戴设备、4种观看条件，共3,250个任务；评测十种开源模型；消融目标尺度、屏幕几何、背景等因素；比较推理时方案（局部缩放、屏幕矫正）与训练时方案PairDistill（跨视图目标表征对齐+坐标监督，训练用独立采集数据，推理不用截图）。

Result: 平均grounding精度由截图94.8%降至相机图像48.5%；尺度、几何、背景变化无法充分解释该退化；局部缩放与屏幕矫正仅缩小部分差距；PairDistill在两个骨干上分别提升9.4与12.2点，可迁移至未见设备与操作系统，结合局部缩放达69.6%与64.4%。

Conclusion: 穿戴视角下的GUI grounding存在显著且未被现有基准覆盖的域差距；配对视图设计与跨视图表征对齐是有效方向，但现有推理与训练手段尚不能弥合差距，需进一步研究。

Abstract: Wearable assistants must localize interface elements from camera observations when direct screenshot access is unavailable, yet existing GUI grounding benchmarks primarily evaluate native screenshots. We introduce GlassGround, a new benchmark that pairs native screenshots with wearable camera observations while holding GUI states, instructions, and targets fixed. GlassGround contains 3,250 tasks across Android and iOS, captured using four wearable devices under four viewing conditions. Across ten open-source models, mean grounding accuracy drops from 94.8% on screenshots to 48.5% on camera images. Further analysis reveals that changes in target scale, screen geometry, and background cannot fully explain this performance degradation. We then study two ways to narrow this gap. At inference time, local zooming and screen rectification help but leave most of the gap. For training-time adaptation, we build on GlassGround's paired-view design and introduce PairDistill, a baseline that aligns target representations across views alongside coordinate supervision. Trained on independently collected data and using no screenshots at inference, it improves camera-image accuracy by 9.4 and 12.2 points on two backbones and transfers to unseen devices and operating systems. Combined with local zooming, it reaches 69.6% and 64.4%, but does not close the gap. The GlassGround dataset and code are available at https://github.com/yunnw/GlassGround.

</details>


### [69] [ScanAR: Parallel Row-Scan Decoding for Efficient Image Generation in Unified Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33761)
*Jingen Qu,Xi Chen,Yingbo Tang,Xinghang Li,Tao Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScanAR 提出“行扫描并行解码”，仅重定义视觉 token 的前驱依赖，使统一多模态自回归模型的可视化解码深度由 HW 降为 H+W，在不改骨干、词表、输出头、不加参数的前提下实现高质量加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
