TL;DR: 论文分析"思考型"视觉语言模型在知识型视觉问答中的两步流程，发现实体识别是瓶颈，且该识别依赖"实体回忆线索"；据此提出ClueRL——一种将优势定位到思维链中实体回忆线索的强化学习方法，在多个实体识别基准及知识型VQA、通用多模态推理上均取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识型VQA需先识别图像实体、再回忆相关知识，"思考型"VLM的推理过程动辄数千token，但其在识别与回忆两个步骤上的表现缺乏系统分析。作者据此展开诊断，以定位瓶颈所在。

Method: 先对四个模型在ViQuAE上进行错误分析，得到两点发现：(1) 识别是瓶颈——76.3%的错误源于实体误识别，其中84.9%在题目中直接给出实体名后即可解决；(2) 识别由"实体回忆线索"（如圣彼得的钥匙这类有助于回忆实体的独特视觉细节）驱动，而非泛化的图像描述。基于此提出ClueRL：在GRPO基础上，将优势（advantage）局部化到思维轨迹中的实体回忆线索处，而非注入新实体知识，从而改变VLM的"思考方式"，促使其更关注视觉细节并发现线索。

Result: ClueRL在三个实体识别基准上优于GRPO及感知奖励基线；线索搜寻行为可泛化到训练任务之外；由于识别是瓶颈，收益延续到知识型VQA，并扩展至通用多模态推理——Qwen3.5-4B在MMStar的ScienceQA与MMMU子集上分别提升4.4和4.0分。

Conclusion: 识别（尤其对实体回忆线索的捕捉）是知识型VQA的关键瓶颈；通过强化学习重塑思维过程而非补充知识，即可有效激活模型已有知识，并带来跨任务的泛化收益。

Abstract: Answering a knowledge-based visual question requires first recognizing the entity in the image and then recalling knowledge about it. Thinking vision-language models (VLMs) reason for thousands of tokens before answering, yet it remains underexplored how well such thinking VLMs perform each of these two steps. We therefore analyze thinking VLMs on this task and make two key observations. (1) Recognition is the bottleneck, since 76.3% of errors across four models on ViQuAE stem from misrecognizing the entity, and 84.9% of these failures are resolved once the entity name is provided in the question. (2) Recognition is driven by entity-recall clues rather than by general descriptions of the image. An entity-recall clue is a distinctive visual detail that helps the model recall the entity, such as keys, a traditional attribute of Saint Peter. Based on these findings, we propose ClueRL, a reinforcement learning approach that extends Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with an advantage localized to the entity-recall clue in the thinking trace. Rather than injecting new entity knowledge, ClueRL changes how VLMs think: it leads them to attend more closely to visual details and spot these clues, helping them use the knowledge they already have. Consequently, it outperforms both GRPO and a perception-reward baseline on three entity recognition benchmarks. Moreover, this clue-seeking behavior generalizes beyond entity recognition, the only task used for training. Since recognition is the bottleneck, the gains carry over to knowledge-based visual question answering. They even extend to general multimodal reasoning, raising Qwen3.5-4B by 4.4 and 4.0 points on the ScienceQA and MMMU subsets of MMStar, respectively.

</details>


### [208] [ShowNow: Fine-Grained Speculative Streaming for Responsive World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34693)
*Yiren Song,Guian Fang,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ShowNow：细粒度投机流式框架，利用播放期算力预先准备动作条件未来，在单张H100上以5B模型实现480p/16fps、8路预备未来，将ViZDoom中位输入到显示延迟从620ms降至208ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界模型的响应性瓶颈不仅在实时吞吐，还在输入延迟：粗粒度生成块会推迟动作更新，而"输入后再生成"又把计算放到响应路径上，导致动作切换响应迟滞。

Method: 提出ShowNow：(1) 单潜变量提交（single-latent commitment）实现高频决策；(2) 可配置预备1–8个动作的未来分支；(3) 构建匹配的多未来数据集，训练从共同状态出发的替代续写；(4) 共享历史树执行与融合提交提升预备效率，把计算移到播放阶段。

Result: 单H100上5B模型于480p达16fps并预备8个未来；ViZDoom中完整部署使中位输入-显示延迟由620ms降至208ms（相对原生分块SolarWM-FT）；匹配策略实验将重启空刻度从1.56%降至0.09%（约94%），并提升对动作变化的准时响应；同时量化了视觉保真度与长时一致性的权衡。

Conclusion: 细粒度提交与提前预备具有互补作用，ShowNow将播放期计算转化为响应式视觉交互，为交互式世界模型的低延迟生成提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Interactive world models must generate video continuously while responding promptly to user actions. Real-time throughput alone leaves input latency unresolved: coarse generation blocks delay action updates, and generation after input adds work to the response path. We introduce ShowNow, a fine-grained speculative streaming framework that uses computation available during playback to prepare action-conditioned futures before input arrives. Single-latent commitment enables frequent decisions, while configurable preparation covers one to eight actions. We construct a matched multi-future dataset to train alternative continuations from a common state; shared-history tree execution and fused commitment make their preparation efficient. On a single H100, our 5B model sustains 480p generation at 16 fps with eight prepared futures. In ViZDoom, the complete ShowNow deployment reduces median input-to-display latency from 620 to 208 ms relative to the native-block SolarWM-FT deployment. Matched-policy experiments reduce restart empty ticks from 1.56% to 0.09% (94%) and improve on-time responses to action changes. These results establish complementary roles for fine-grained commitment and advance preparation, with measured trade-offs in visual fidelity and long-horizon consistency. ShowNow turns playback-time computation into responsive visual interaction. Project: https://github.com/showlab/ShowNow.

</details>


### [209] [Seeing the Right Context: Grounded On-Policy Distillation for Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34700)
*Yichang Luo,Hourun Zhu,Shixiong Liu,Kan Wei,Shi Liang,Yidan Zhang,Lei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeOPD 通过构建显式编码空间上下文关系的 GeoContext-5K 数据集，并采用基于区域标记与视觉事实的证据恢复式在线策略蒸馏，提升遥感多模态大模型的空间推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感场景理解要求模型结合邻近目标与周边结构来解释被查询目标，但这些上下文关系在标准答案与边界框中往往隐含不显式，导致模型缺乏空间参照约束。

Method: 构建 GeoContext-5K（5,394 条 VQA 训练对，含空间线索、视觉事实、支撑区域）；在在线策略蒸馏中使用冻结教师模型，对完整图像叠加区域标记并提供视觉事实并保留周边空间参照；优先采信与学生"证据恢复后下一 token 分布偏移"一致的教师纠正；辅以第二目标，按遮蔽使教师对同一采样 token 对数概率下降的幅度来加权学生对两个视图评分的对比。

Result: 在五个遥感 VQA 基准上优于所评估的开源 MLLM 及匹配的后训练基线，相对 4B 与 9B 基座模型平均分别提升 5.81 与 2.97 个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式建模范式结合证据感知的蒸馏权重设计能有效注入空间上下文推理能力，数据集与代码将公开。

Abstract: Remote sensing scene understanding requires multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to interpret queried targets in relation to nearby objects and surrounding structures, which serve as spatial references or define the scope of an answer. However, standard answers and bounding boxes often leave these contextual relationships implicit. Thus, we construct GeoContext-5K to make them explicit in 5,394 training pairs for visual question answering (VQA), with spatial cues, visual facts, and supporting regions. GeOPD uses these annotations for on-policy distillation, guiding a frozen teacher with region marks on the full image and a visual fact while preserving surrounding spatial references. It prioritizes teacher corrections that agree with the shift in the student's next token distribution when evidence is restored. A complementary objective weights the contrast between the student's scores on the two views by how much masking lowers the teacher's log probability for the same sampled token. Experiments on five remote sensing VQA benchmarks show that GeOPD outperforms the evaluated open-source MLLMs and matched post-training baselines, improving over the 4B and 9B base models by 5.81 and 2.97 percentage points on average. The dataset and code will be publicly released at https://github.com/YeeCharm/GeOPD.

</details>


### [210] [Reading, Not Adapting: Unlocking Frozen Visual Knowledge for AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34719)
*Yuncheng Guo,Ying Dong,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReMoT：受Mixture-of-Transformers启发，在完全冻结的视觉基础模型内，从空间对齐的RGB与小波两个视图读取互补知识，仅用3,074个可训练参数在八组基准上取得91.2%平均准确率，验证了"结构化知识读出"优于骨干网络适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向未见图像生成器的泛化需求推动了对预训练视觉表征的检测适配，但近期研究发现视觉基础模型本身已编码可迁移的检测知识，进一步适配反而损害泛化性。因此需要从"骨干适配"转向"知识读出"。然而常规线性探测只能利用末层RGB特征；多线索检测器虽扩展证据来源，却存在适配破坏预训练可迁移性、异构编码器特征融合困难、晚期融合缺乏中间层跨视图交互等缺陷。

Method: ReMoT（Mixture-of-Transformers式框架）：在完全冻结的视觉基础模型中，从空间对齐的RGB视图与小波视图读取互补知识；采用分阶段联合注意力实现可控的局部与全局跨视图交互；引入可学习的融合查询（fusion queries）沿Transformer深度逐层读取互补证据，且不回写（no writeback）以保持预训练表征不被破坏。

Result: 在八个基准组上取得91.2%的平均准确率，仅有3,074个可训练参数，在评估方法中取得最高总体平均性能。

Conclusion: 结果支持将结构化知识读出作为骨干适配的一种有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: Generalization to unseen image generators has driven efforts to adapt pretrained visual representations for detection. Yet recent findings show that vision foundation models already encode transferable detection knowledge, while further adaptation can impair generalization. This motivates a shift from backbone adaptation to knowledge readout. Conventional linear probing offers a direct way to read this knowledge but is limited to final-layer RGB features. Multi-cue detectors broaden the evidence base, but their designs introduce other tradeoffs: adaptation may compromise pretrained transferability, heterogeneous encoders complicate feature integration, and late fusion precludes intermediate cross-view interaction. To overcome these limitations, we propose ReMoT, a Mixture-of-Transformers-inspired framework that reads complementary knowledge from spatially aligned RGB and wavelet views within a fully frozen visual foundation. ReMoT employs staged joint attention to enable controlled local and global cross-view interaction, while learnable fusion queries progressively read complementary evidence across Transformer depth without writeback. ReMoT achieves 91.2% average accuracy across eight benchmark groups with 3,074 trainable parameters, attaining the highest overall average among the evaluated methods. These results support structured knowledge readout as a promising alternative to backbone adaptation. Code is available at https://github.com/yunncheng/ReMoT.

</details>


### [211] [LiDAR Intensity Reconstruction with Limited Real Data: An Architecture Benchmark under Fixed-Epoch Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34724)
*Christopher M. Coovrey,Aniruddha Bora*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文以统一20轮训练协议在SemanticKITTI与VoxelScape距离图像上基准测试9种神经架构，比较LiDAR强度重建效果，并考察0%–100%真实数据混合比例下DDPM与UNet-V2的取舍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实LiDAR强度测量稀缺，物理仿真数据可作补充，但其增益依赖网络架构与真实数据量，缺乏系统性对比。

Method: 以深度、语义标签、入射角与材质反射率为输入重建强度；9种架构，固定20 epoch（非算力对齐）；指标含MAE、MSE、PSNR、SSIM、推理耗时及频谱能量一致性；另对4种架构测试0%、1%、5%、10%、20%、50%、100%真实帧的固定规模混合训练。

Result: 纯真实数据下UNet-V2指标最优（MAE 0.234、MSE 0.140、PSNR 8.64 dB、SSIM 0.647），DDPM次之；在含1%–10%真实数据的若干混合设定中DDPM误差更低、图像质量指标更高；频谱分析显示频带差异依架构而异。

Conclusion: 研究刻画了既定数据配比与固定轮次协议下的重建权衡，未证明算力对齐下的优越性，也未将扩散目标与骨干网络差异分离。

Abstract: Physics-derived simulation can supplement scarce real-world LiDAR intensity measurements, but its effectiveness depends on the learning architecture and the amount of real training data available. We benchmark nine neural architectures for LiDAR intensity reconstruction from depth, semantic labels, incidence angle, and material reflectance, using real SemanticKITTI and synthetic VoxelScape range-image data. All models follow a fixed 20-epoch protocol; this controls the number of training passes, not the computational cost across architectures. We report mean absolute error (MAE), mean squared error (MSE), peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR), structural similarity (SSIM), inference timing, and spectral-energy agreement. We also evaluate four selected architectures with fixed-size training mixtures containing 0\%, 1\%, 5\%, 10\%, 20\%, 50\%, or 100\% real frames. With real-only training, UNet-V2 achieves the best reported reconstruction metrics (MAE 0.234, MSE 0.140, PSNR 8.64 dB, and SSIM 0.647), followed by a denoising diffusion model (DDPM). In several mixtures containing 1--10\% real data, DDPM achieves lower error and higher image-quality metrics than the evaluated deterministic alternatives, whereas UNet-V2 leads with 100\% real data. Spectral analysis additionally reveals architecture-dependent discrepancies across frequency bands. These findings characterize reconstruction trade-offs under the stated data mixtures and fixed-epoch protocol, without establishing compute-matched superiority or isolating the diffusion objective from backbone differences.

</details>


### [212] [WorldInteract: Controllable Streaming Video Generation for Multi-Material Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34730)
*Ahmed Hatem,Caoliwen Wang,Robert Hoang,Siyuan Chen,Spencer Szabados,Zike Wu,Xiyang Tan,Shenyifan Lu,Rafael Bischof,Bernd Bickel,Renjie Liao,Peter Yichen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldInteract：将跨材质交互分解为"材质相关行为生成"与"接触响应计算"的流式视频生成框架，通过共享速度增量接口复用无交互视频中学到的材质行为，并用Material-Aware RL以可验证物理奖励精修。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可控视频生成中材质相关的动力学研究不足，导致在含多种材质的场景中难以实现可靠控制；同时缺乏覆盖多样材质配对与接触构型的监督接触视频。

Method: 提出流式视频生成框架WorldInteract，把跨材质交互解耦为两部分：（1）物理控制器估计质量与恢复系数，预测接触并计算速度增量；（2）因果生成器利用学到的材质行为执行这些增量。外部推力与接触冲量统一表示为共享速度增量接口，使从无交互视频学到的材质行为可迁移至交互场景。生成器在22类材质、3万条仿真无交互视频上训练，并引入Material-Aware RL，用可验证物理奖励优化材质行为与控制执行。

Result: 在Physics-IQ上显著优于现有SOTA方法，并在真实世界图像上实现更可靠的推力控制。

Conclusion: 通过行为生成与接触响应的显式分离，可在无监督接触视频条件下实现跨材质交互控制，RL精修进一步提升了物理真实性与可控性。

Abstract: Video world models aim to create interactive physical worlds that capture how diverse materials move and interact. Yet material-dependent dynamics remain underexplored in controllable video generation, limiting reliable control in scenes with different materials. We present WorldInteract, a streaming video generation framework that decomposes cross-material interaction into material-dependent behavior generation and contact response computation. External pushes and contact impulses are expressed through a shared velocity-increment interface, allowing material behaviors learned from interaction-free videos to be reused in interacting scenes. A physics controller predicts contacts and computes velocity increments from estimated masses and restitution coefficients, while a causal generator executes these increments using learned material behaviors. This separation enables cross-material interaction without supervised contact videos spanning diverse material pairings and contact configurations. We train the generator on 30k simulated interaction-free videos spanning 22 material classes and introduce Material-Aware RL, using verifiable physical rewards to refine material behavior and control execution. WorldInteract substantially outperforms state-of-the-art methods on Physics-IQ and delivers more reliable push control on real-world images.

</details>


### [213] [Was the Model Given the Right Action? Auditing and Repairing Action Interfaces in Navigation World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34739)
*Ajmain Istiak Apon,Md Mehedi Hasan,Sejuti Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文审计了Navigation World Models（NWM）训练与推演阶段动作编码不一致的问题，发现并修正三处交互性差异后，无需重训练即可将平均视觉预测误差（DreamSim，RECON数据集）降低42%；并提出工具ActRosetta用于检测、诊断和修复动作编码不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型通常以“从动作预测未来观测”的方式评估，但评估假设动作的准备方式与训练时一致；实际上未被检查的输入不匹配可能使模型显得比真实情况更不准确，导致评估失真与模型排序错误。

Method: 审计NWM的训练代码与rollout代码，识别出三处相互作用的差异；在修正输入（不重训练）后以DreamSim在RECON导航数据集上测量视觉预测误差，并对比仅修正归一化偏移的效果；另外对两个替代模型人为注入编码错误以检验敏感性；最后提出ActRosetta工具，用于检查动作编码与训练是否匹配、识别不一致的可支持原因并在信息充分时修复输入。

Result: 修正输入后平均视觉预测误差降低42%；修正还改变了探索性分析中NWM各checkpoint的排序，并消除或削弱了两个替代模型的测量优势；仅修正归一化偏移反而使误差上升，说明交互性不匹配必须一并检查；向两个替代模型注入编码错误后二者精度均下降，表明对输入准备的敏感性不限于NWM。

Conclusion: 对任何动作条件世界模型而言，应在报告rollout误差之前验证评估阶段的动作编码与训练完全一致。ActRosetta能检测、诊断并部分修复不一致，但其修复在预测对动作响应微弱的场景作用有限、诊断覆盖范围有限、改进控制效果尚未得到证明。

Abstract: World models are evaluated by predicting future observations from actions. These evaluations assume that actions are prepared the same way as during training, but an unchecked mismatch can make a model appear less accurate than it is. We audit this assumption in Navigation World Models (NWM) and find three interacting differences between its training and rollout code. Correcting the inputs, without retraining, reduces mean visual prediction error by 42%, measured with DreamSim on the RECON navigation dataset. The correction also changes the ranking of NWM checkpoints in an exploratory analysis and removes or reduces two alternative models' measured advantages. Correcting only the normalization offset instead increases error, showing why interacting mismatches must be checked together. To test whether sensitivity to input preparation extends beyond NWM, we deliberately introduce encoding errors into two alternative models; both become less accurate. We introduce ActRosetta, a tool that checks whether action encoding matches training, identifies supported causes of disagreement, and repairs inputs when sufficient information is available. Repair helps little where predictions barely respond to actions, the tool's diagnosis has limited coverage, and improved control remains unproven. For any action-conditioned world model, the lesson is the same: before reporting rollout error, verify that evaluation encodes actions exactly as training did.

</details>


### [214] [Comparative Study of Deep Learning Architectures for Brain Tumour Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34781)
*Prisha Gupta,Pradeep Kumar Gupta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究在四源合并的MRI脑肿瘤数据集上比较六种深度学习架构，并引入去重（MD5与感知哈希）与分组交叉验证的防泄漏流程及留一数据集（LODO）跨数据集测试；结果显示分布内准确率高达98%以上，但跨数据集泛化显著下降（最优EfficientNet-B0仅78.4%），说明仅报告同分布精度会高估真实性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多已发表的脑肿瘤MRI深度学习研究仅在小型单源数据集上训练与测试，所报准确率难以在数据集之外成立，缺乏对数据泄漏和跨数据集泛化的严格评估。

Method: 构建由四个公开MRI数据集合并而成的语料，进行四分类（健康、脑膜瘤、胶质瘤、垂体瘤）。比较六种架构：基线CNN、注意力CNN、ShuffleNet，以及引入CBAM的EfficientNet-B0、ResNet-50与Xception。设计防泄漏流程：用MD5哈希去除精确重复（550张），用感知哈希去除近重复（8,024张），并在5折分组交叉验证中将视觉相似图像划入同一折；另用完全留出的数据集做LODO测试以衡量跨数据集泛化，并以Grad-CAM进行可解释性分析。

Result: 常规随机划分下ShuffleNet以1.2M参数达97.1%准确率；防泄漏分组K折下ShuffleNetV2（98.41%）与EfficientNet-B0（98.23%）无统计显著差异（配对t检验，p=0.19），且均显著优于自建CNN。留出数据集上所有模型精度下降：EfficientNet-B0泛化最佳（78.4%），ShuffleNetV2降至66.2%。主要错误模式为胶质瘤—脑膜瘤混淆，Grad-CAM显示预测由肿瘤区域驱动。

Conclusion: 分布内准确率会高估真实世界性能，防泄漏评估与跨数据集评估应与常规精度一并报告；模型选择需兼顾参数效率与泛化能力。

Abstract: Accurate and early classification of brain tumours from magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is essential for treatment planning, yet many published deep learning studies are trained and tested on small, single-source datasets, reporting accuracies that may not hold outside these datasets. This study compares six deep learning architectures for four-class classification (healthy, meningioma, glioma and pituitary tumour): a baseline convolutional neural network (CNN), an attention-based CNN, ShuffleNet, and EfficientNet-B0, ResNet-50 and Xception augmented with the Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM). The models were trained on a corpus assembled from four publicly available MRI datasets. Because these datasets carry no patient identifiers, a leakage-aware pipeline was developed that removes exact duplicates using MD5 hashing (550 images) and near-duplicates using perceptual hashing (8,024 images), and assigns visually similar images to the same fold during 5-fold group cross-validation. A leave-one-dataset-out (LODO) test on a fully held-out dataset was used to measure cross-dataset generalisation. Under a conventional random split, ShuffleNet achieved 97.1% accuracy with 1.2M parameters. Under leakage-aware group K-fold cross-validation, ShuffleNetV2 (98.41%) and EfficientNet-B0 (98.23%) were statistically indistinguishable (paired t-test, p = 0.19), and both significantly outperformed the custom CNNs. On the held-out dataset, accuracy dropped for every model: EfficientNet-B0 generalised best (78.4%), while ShuffleNetV2 fell to 66.2%. Glioma-meningioma confusion was the dominant error mode, and Grad-CAM maps showed that predictions were driven by tumour regions. These results indicate that in-distribution accuracy alone overstates real-world performance and that leakage-aware and cross-dataset evaluation should be reported alongside it.

</details>


### [215] [Generation as Reverse Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34782)
*Cheng Shi,Sibei Yang,Hanzhong Guo,Yipeng Qin,Pietro Lio,Yizhou Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RevFlow 提出"生成即逆向编码"原则：以冻结编码器的中间表征为逐级生成目标，并设计统一生成模型 HyC-DiT 完成多阶段过渡，在极少训练轮次下实现高质量、少步采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉表征虽加速生成学习，但抽象掉了外观与空间细节，使回到像素的过程负担转移到大型解码器上；作者质疑：既然编码器已提供"像素→语义"的学习路径，为何"表征→像素"路径还要独立学习。

Method: 将生成视为逆向编码：先生成深层语义表征，再生成保留外观与空间细节的浅层表征，最后解码到像素，逐级以冻结编码器的中间表征为生成目标。为避免为每阶段单独训练生成器，提出 HyC-DiT——共享标准 DiT 主干、按阶段注入条件的统一生成模型；并指出在语义表征空间中的原生匹配（native matching）可实现少步生成。

Result: 加速学习与采样：ImageNet 256×256 上仅训练 5 个 epoch 即达 690M 模型 1.96 FID、1.55B 模型 1.74 FID；89M 解码器取得 30.25 dB 重建 PSNR；经短暂 native-matching 适配后，RevFlow-XL 在每阶段单步采样下达到 2.85 FID。

Conclusion: 复用编码器的语义编码路径作为生成目标的逆过程，可在统一骨干下实现高效、少步的高质量图像生成与重建，显著降低训练成本与解码器负担。

Abstract: Pretrained visual representations accelerate generative learning by abstracting away appearance and spatial detail. Yet the same abstraction makes the return to pixels far harder, shifting much of the burden onto a larger decoder. We ask why the representation-to-pixel path should be learned independently when the encoder already provides a learned pixel-to-semantics path (the semantic encoding path). We introduce RevFlow, based on the principle of generation as reverse encoding. RevFlow uses intermediate representations of a frozen encoder as successive generative targets, first generating deep semantic representations, then shallow representations that retain appearance and spatial detail, before decoding to pixels. To learn these transitions without training a separate generator for each stage, we develop HyC-DiT, a unified generative model with a standard DiT backbone shared across stages and stage-specific condition injection. We further show that native matching in semantic representation space enables few-step generation. RevFlow accelerates both learning and sampling. On ImageNet $256 \times 256$, RevFlow reaches 1.96 FID at 690M and 1.74 FID at 1.55B after only 5 training epochs. It also achieves 30.25 dB reconstruction PSNR with an 89M decoder. After a short native-matching adaptation phase, RevFlow-XL achieves 2.85 FID using a single sampling step in each stage. Our code repository is at https://github.com/ChengShiest/RevFlow

</details>


### [216] [SceneDesigner3D: Agentic Evolutionary Search for Principled Rearrangement of 3D Indoor Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34783)
*Chandan Yeshwanth,Angela Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SceneDesigner3D，一种将3D室内场景重排表示为可编辑程序并结合VLM智能体与进化搜索的方法，生成多样、美观且功能合理的重排方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D内容创作需要场景重排，但要求结果同时具备多样性、美学性与功能性，传统方法难以系统探索设计空间。

Method: 将重排编码为可编辑程序，维护候选布局种群并进化；从多个不同设计思路出发，VLM智能体通过精修候选、探索新策略或从零重设计来变异程序，同时验证物理合法性；由VLM评判器按提示符合度及平衡、分区、强调等设计原则打分，并辅以 circulation、杂乱度等数值化指标；维护结构多样的优质候选档案以提升多样性。

Result: 在 SAGE-10k 与 SceneSmith 数据集上，提示符合度最高提升5.1%，计算设计原则得分最高提升15.2%。

Conclusion: 将程序化表示、VLM智能体与进化搜索相结合，可有效提升3D场景重排的功能性与审美质量，并保持方案多样性。

Abstract: Scene rearrangement is an essential task for 3D content creation applications, requiring generation of diverse, aesthetic and functional scene rearrangements. We present SceneDesigner3D, an agentic evolutionary search method for 3D indoor scene rearrangement that systematically explores the design space of 3D scenes and generates multiple solutions according to common interior design principles. Given an input scene and a text prompt, SceneDesigner3D represents the rearrangement as an editable program and evolves a population of candidate layouts. Starting from distinct design ideas, a VLM agent mutates the programs by refining promising candidates, exploring new rearrangement strategies or redesigning from scratch, while verifying the layouts for physical validity. Proposals are scored by a VLM judge for adherence to the input prompt and satisfaction of design principles such as balance, zoning and emphasis, as well as numerically computed principles such as circulation and clutter. An archive of top-performing, structurally varied candidates further increases diversity of rearrangements. Experiments on the SAGE-10k and SceneSmith datasets show that SceneDesigner3D improves prompt adherence and computed design principle scores over baselines by up to 5.1% and 15.2%, respectively. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [217] [CIRKDV2: Cross-Image Relational Knowledge Distillation with Contextual Modeling for Efficient Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34795)
*Chuanguang Yang,Yu Wang,Chengqing Yu,Xinqiang Yu,Weilun Feng,Yuqi Li,Zhulin An,Libo Huang,Boyu Diao,Fei Wang,Fuzhen Zhuang,Yongjun Xu,Yingli Tian,Tingwen Huang,Yongduan Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CIRKDV2，将知识蒸馏从单图结构化信息扩展到跨图像的全局像素-像素、像素-区域、通道-通道关系，并辅以上下文特征蒸馏缓解语义鸿沟，在多个分割基准上大幅超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义分割KD方法仅引导学生模仿教师由单张图像生成的结构化信息，忽略了数据集中跨图像的全局语义关系，而这些关系对蒸馏有价值；同时传统特征蒸馏存在师生语义层级不一致造成的语义鸿沟问题。

Method: 跨图像关系蒸馏：从数据集全局图像建模像素-像素、像素-区域、通道-通道三类结构关系并迁移给学生；上下文特征蒸馏：用上下文聚合增强学生像素嵌入，使其语义层级与教师匹配，从而避免语义鸿沟。

Result: 在Cityscapes、ADE20K、Pascal VOC、COCO-Stuff-164K上，跨多种网络架构，CIRKDV2均大幅优于现有语义分割蒸馏SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 将蒸馏信号从单图结构化信息扩展至跨图像全局关系，并配合上下文聚合的语义层级对齐，可有效提升学生特征表示能力，是一种通用且有效的分割蒸馏范式。

Abstract: Existing Knowledge Distillation (KD) methods for semantic segmentation often guide the student to mimic the teacher's structured information generated from an individual image. However, they ignore the global semantic relations across various images that are valuable for KD. This paper proposes Cross-Image Relational KD V2 (CIRKDV2), which focuses on transferring structured pixel-to-pixel, pixel-to-region, and channel-to-channel relations modeled from global images in the dataset. A large teacher network often constructs a well-structured feature space in terms of global contextual dependencies. CIRKDV2 makes the student mimic the better structured semantic relations of the teacher, thus improving the feature representational capability. Beyond relational distillation, we further propose contextual feature distillation by using context aggregation to augment student pixel embeddings to match the semantic level with the teacher. This avoids the semantic gap problem between the teacher and student occurred in traditional feature distillation studies. Experimental results on Cityscapes, ADE20K, Pascal VOC, and COCO-Stuff-164K demonstrate that CIRKDV2 outperforms the state-of-the-art semantic segmentation distillation methods by large margins across various network architectures. Our code and pre-trained models are publicly available at https://github.com/winycg/CIRKD.

</details>


### [218] [Guidance as Variance Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34801)
*Ali Backour,Artem Lukoianov,Frank Permenter,Tommi Jaakkola,Justin Solomon,Vincent Sitzmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文重新解释扩散模型引导（guidance）机制：引导并不改善后验均值预测（反而变差），但能恢复预测方差；提出"方差匹配引导"闭式规则，无需调参即可复现11个主流模型的手选引导系数，并能自动生成噪声级调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引导显著提升扩散模型采样质量，但其作用机制长期缺乏清晰解释。作者从"引导是否真的改善了模型预测目标（后验均值）"这一反直觉问题切入，试图给出可验证、可预测的机制性解释。

Method: (1) 实验检验引导对后验均值预测误差与预测方差的影响；(2) 提出方差匹配引导（variance-matched guidance）：以引导后预测方差匹配数据方差为准则，闭式求解引导尺度；(3) 按噪声级逐级应用得到无需调参的引导调度；(4) 提出"随机误差重缩放"机制解释，并据此推导两个可检验假设（负引导尺度可行、自引导需更大尺度）。

Result: 在实践中常用尺度下，引导使后验均值预测变差但样本变好；预测方差低于数据方差而引导可恢复之。方差匹配规则无需调参即复现图像、视频、音频、运动与世界生成共11个广泛使用模型的手选引导尺度（此前解释均无法预测）；逐噪声级应用在 SD 3.5 Medium 上生成的调度优于手工调度；实验支持负尺度可行与自引导需更大尺度两个假设。

Conclusion: 引导的本质并非提升后验均值精度，而是将各模型预测误差之差重缩放为具有后验协方差的样本，从而改善采样。方差匹配提供了一种无需调参、可跨模态预测引导尺度的实用准则，并给出可检验的机制假说。

Abstract: Guidance repels the prediction of a diffusion model away from a weaker reference prediction. It substantially improves sample quality, but its mechanism remains poorly understood. We first find that, at the scales used in practice, guidance does not improve the prediction of the posterior mean (the model's training target); it makes the prediction worse, while samples improve. Second, posterior-mean predictions have less variance than the data, and we find that guidance restores it. We introduce variance-matched guidance: a closed-form rule that picks the guidance scale by matching the variance of the guided predictions to that of the data. Without tuning, it recovers the hand-picked scales of eleven widely used models across image, video, audio, motion, and world generation; to our knowledge, no previous explanation of guidance predicts them. Applied per noise level, it yields a schedule without tuned hyperparameters that users prefer to hand-crafted ones on SD 3.5 Medium. Third, we offer an interpretation of the mechanism behind guidance: each model makes a random error in predicting the posterior mean, and guidance rescales the difference of these errors into a sample with the posterior's covariance, which improves sampling. This view also suggests two new hypotheses that our experiments support: guidance can work with negative scales, and guiding a model with its own checkpoint needs a larger scale than guiding it with an independently trained model.

</details>


### [219] [WorldExpand: Building Coherent 3D Worlds via Sparse Joint Panoramic Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34810)
*Yuyang Yin,Chenjie Cao,Xuhui Zuo,Yuning Gong,Pengfei Wang,Junta Wu,Zhenwei Wang,Yao Zhao,Tengfei Wang,Yunchao Wei,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldExpand 将单张全景图的场景扩展重新定义为"稀疏、互补、高分辨率全景观测"的生成，通过覆盖引导视角选择与联合全景扩散，实现快速、跨视角一致的高质量三维世界构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张全景图构建完整三维世界时，场景大部分区域未被观测。现有全景视频方法靠密集序列生成扩大空间覆盖，但相邻帧冗余大、长轨迹误差累积、独立生成的方向之间不一致。

Method: 将扩展任务建模为稀疏互补高分辨率全景观测的生成。1) Coverage-Guided View Selection：用场景自适应象限与基于 warp 的覆盖分数确定信息量高的目标视角位置（"在哪观测"）；2) Joint Panoramic Diffusion：利用基于高斯泼溅的几何 warp 联合合成四张位姿锚定、跨视角一致的全景图（"如何合成"）；3) 诊断引导的全局—局部注意力调度，仅在下层敏感层保留全局注意力，降低高分辨率联合生成的训练与推理开销。所得带位姿全景图可直接输入现有重建流水线构建显式三维表示。

Result: 实验表明能恢复遮挡物背后和远处转角处的内容，在多个数据集上提升新视角合成与下游三维重建指标，并稳健泛化到多样真实场景；速度显著快于 SOTA 全景视频方法，同时分辨率更高、跨视角一致性更强。

Conclusion: 稀疏互补观测是替代密集序列扩展的有效表示，WorldExpand 在效率、分辨率与一致性上均优于现有方法，可作为通用三维世界构建前端。

Abstract: Constructing complete 3D worlds from a single panorama is challenging because large portions of the scene remain unobserved. Existing panoramic-video methods expand spatial coverage through dense sequential generation, but neighboring frames contain substantial redundancy, long trajectories accumulate errors, and independently generated directions may be inconsistent. We present WorldExpand, which formulates single-panorama world expansion as the generation of sparse, complementary, and high-resolution panoramic observations. This representation concentrates the generation budget on informative scene regions and preserves the fine-grained details required for 3D construction. WorldExpand addresses two key challenges: where to observe and how to synthesize. Our Coverage-Guided View Selection identifies informative target locations using scene-adaptive quadrants and warp-based coverage scores, while our Joint Panoramic Diffusion uses Gaussian-splatting-based geometric warps to jointly synthesize four pose-grounded and cross-view-consistent panoramas. A diagnostic-guided global--local attention schedule retains global attention only in sensitive layers, reducing the training and inference costs of high-resolution joint generation. The resulting posed panoramas can be directly fed into an existing reconstruction pipeline to build an explicit 3D representation. Experiments show that WorldExpand recovers content behind occluders and around distant corners, improves both novel-view synthesis and downstream 3D reconstruction metrics across various datasets, and generalizes robustly to diverse in-the-wild scenes. WorldExpand runs significantly faster than the state-of-the-art panoramic-video method while producing higher-resolution observations with stronger cross-view consistency.

</details>


### [220] [HOG-WAM: Improving Visual Robustness through Future Gradient Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34811)
*Boxuan Dong,Yupeng Qian,Ying Wu,Heshuai Li,Junhao Tan,Zhenxiong Tan,Weijia Wu,Weihao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HOG-WAM 将未来方向梯度直方图（HOG）作为结构监督信号，通过辅助 DiT 模块以非对称注意力约束视觉表示，从而提升世界-动作模型在视觉损坏下的控制鲁棒性；该模块仅训练时使用，推理时移除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型联合预测未来观测与动作，但视觉损坏会破坏其生成的策略，导致鲁棒性不足；需要在训练中引入来自演示图像的结构性监督以增强视觉表示的鲁棒性。

Method: 从演示图像直接提取未来 HOG 作为结构监督；引入辅助 HOG 扩散 Transformer（DiT），通过非对称注意力读取 RGB 特征，使 HOG 预测目标反向塑造用于控制的视觉表示；模块仅在训练阶段使用，推理时移除，保持原 FastWAM 的 RGB-to-action 架构；构建 RoboTwin-Plus 评估四种受控观测损坏。

Result: RoboTwin-Plus 上四种损坏下成功率均为最高，混合噪声下由 78.86% 提升至 82.86%，干净场景性能相当；LIBERO Plus 总体成功率 74.49%→75.26%，传感器噪声下 74.14%→78.89%；四个 Franka 真实任务平均成功率干净 40%→59%、噪声 17%→24%；消融表明 RGB 观测+辅助 HOG 预测优于以 HOG 替换 RGB。

Conclusion: 未来梯度预测可作为视觉鲁棒控制的互补监督信号，且在物理任务上的绝对增益大于仿真，验证了结构监督与 RGB 表示融合的有效性。

Abstract: World-action models jointly predict future observations and actions, yet visual corruption can disrupt the resulting policies. We introduce HOG-WAM, which adds future Histograms of Oriented Gradients (HOG) as structural supervision derived directly from demonstration images. An auxiliary HOG diffusion transformer (DiT) module reads RGB features through asymmetric attention, allowing its objective to shape the visual representation used for control. The module is used only during training and removed at inference, preserving FastWAM's RGB-to-action architecture. We evaluate four controlled observation corruptions through a visual-corruption extension of RoboTwin, termed RoboTwin-Plus; HOG-WAM achieves the highest success among the compared policies under all four. Mixed-noise success increases from 78.86% to 82.86% relative to FastWAM, with comparable performance on clean scenes. On LIBERO Plus, overall success increases from 74.49% to 75.26%, with the largest gain under sensor noise, from 74.14% to 78.89%. Across four Franka tasks, average success increases from 40% to 59% under clean observations and from 17% to 24% under noisy observations. A representation ablation favors combining RGB observations with auxiliary HOG prediction over replacing RGB with HOG. These findings support future-gradient prediction as complementary supervision for visually robust control, with larger absolute gains on physical tasks than in simulation. Code is available at https://github.com/iPPLab/HOG-WAM.

</details>


### [221] [Solve Vision with Code: Coding Agents Are Stronger Visual Reasoners Than Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34813)
*Dezhi Luo,Fengyuan Yu,Haotian Zhang,Zehong Zhao,Ran Ji,Siyuan An,Huayi Qin,Qingying Gao,Zhongang Cai,Lei Yang,Ziqi Huang,Ruisi Wang,Maijunxian Wang,Hokin Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在相同输入、输出规范与评估器条件下，用编码智能体编写渲染视频的程序，与视频生成模型在同一基准上对比；结果显示程序综合（最高0.923）显著优于视频生成（最强基线低0.22以上），说明可计算任务上程序合成是更可靠的视觉推理路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型被视为"原生视觉推理器"——推理直接在帧中展开而非经由语言或代码，该范式被默认更优，但从未在同等条件下与符号化替代方案（程序合成）对照检验。

Method: 构建受控对比：同一任务、相同首帧与提示、相同输出规范与同一未修改的规则评估器。编码智能体不生成像素，而是编写程序渲染答案视频；在VBVR-Pro-Bench（500实例、五类认知任务、带符号真值）上进行，智能体无真值/元数据/生成模型访问权限，每例一次尝试。另将程序区分为"描绘性程序"（导航图像以获得可迁移方案）与"命题性程序"（基于硬编码数值运算）。

Result: 三个闭源智能体（Codex+gpt-6-astra、Claude Code+Fable 5.1、Gemini CLI+Gemini 3.1 Pro）分别得分0.923、0.894、0.893，均高出最强视频模型0.22以上；23个可运行开源权重模型中最佳者GLM-5为0.572，超过除RL训练版以外的所有视频模型。

Conclusion: 对于解可由首帧与提示计算得到的任务，程序合成目前是比视频生成显著更可靠的视觉推理路径；描绘性/命题性程序的区分揭示了智能体程序中视觉推理的展开方式及其对推理本质的启示。

Abstract: Video generation models are increasingly evaluated as visual reasoners: given a first frame and a natural-language prompt, can the model produce the video in which the specified task is carried out? The paradigm is considered advantageous because the reasoning is native to the visual medium, unfolding directly in the frames rather than through language or code. Whether this native route is in fact the more reliable path to visual reasoning has not been tested against a symbolic alternative under the same conditions. We therefore pose the same tasks, with identical inputs, output specification and evaluator, to both media: rather than denoising pixels, a coding agent writes a program that renders the answer video, which an unmodified rule-based evaluator scores. On VBVR-Pro-Bench (Xu et al., 2026), a 500-instance suite spanning five cognitive categories with symbolic ground truth, the agent receives the same first frame and prompt as the video model, with no access to ground truth, metadata or generation models, and one attempt per instance. Three closed-model agents (Codex with gpt-6-astra, Claude Code with Fable 5.1 and Gemini CLI with Gemini 3.1 Pro) score 0.923, 0.894 and 0.893, each more than 0.22 above the strongest video model. Among 23 runnable open-weight models, the best, GLM-5, reaches 0.572, above all video models except the RL-trained one. Together, we show that for tasks whose solutions can be computed from a first frame and a prompt, program synthesis is currently a substantially more reliable route to visual reasoning than video generation. We further distinguish depictive programs, which navigate the image to recover a transferable scheme, from propositional programs, which operate on hard-coded values, revealing how visual reasoning unfolds in agent-written programs and what it implies for its nature of reasoning in general.

</details>


### [222] [Beyond Feature Fusion: Sensor-Grounded Semantic-Spiking Learning for Robust Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34828)
*Hanwen Liang,Wenxuan Liu,Wenxin Huang,Jingling Yuan,Zhaofei Yu,Xian Zhong,Tiejun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SPIRE：一种基于传感器物理特性的语义-脉冲框架，仅用单条脉冲流实现光照鲁棒目标检测，通过外观视图与累积视图双分支、ANN引导脉冲编码及对比度残差修正，在仿真VOC/COCO过/欠曝场景与真实PKU-Vidar-DVS数据上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统帧相机在欠曝时丢失物体细节、过曝时模糊边界，难以应对极端光照；脉冲相机将光强编码为高频二值流，长窗口脉冲计数提供稳定外观线索、短观测可从稀疏测量中反映发放率变化，但缺乏能同时利用这两种特性的检测方法。

Method: SPIRE从单个脉冲包构建两路信息：1）外观视图——用完整数据包经面向检测的光照自适应生成；2）累积视图序列——由逐渐加长的前缀构建，每个前缀给出率图、边缘图及刻画脉冲累积变化的率差。在选定尺度上，将ANN特征与脉冲导出的初始化联合用于设置脉冲神经元的初始膜电位和发放阈值，使语义线索在时间编码中塑造响应；训练时以局部脉冲计数对比度监督两分支。推理时将实测对比度与ANN预测对比度之间的残差结合脉冲特征，对融合特征金字塔施加有界修正。模型记为SPIRE-D。

Result: 在仿真VOC与COCO的四种欠/过曝设置下，SPIRE-D较最强基线提升4.00–5.50 AP50；在真实PKU-Vidar-DVS录制数据上达到67.07 AP50与40.10 AP75；计算量比SPFD减少50.4% MACs。

Conclusion: 将脉冲相机的物理编码特性与ANN语义特征深度耦合（初始化脉冲参数＋对比度残差修正）可在极端光照下实现高精度、低计算量的目标检测，验证了"传感器接地"的语义-脉冲混合范式的有效性。

Abstract: Underexposure weakens object details, and overexposure obscures boundaries. Spike cameras encode light into high-frequency binary streams. Long-window spike counts give stable appearance cues, while short observations reveal changes in firing rates from sparse measurements. We propose SPIRE, a sensor-grounded semantic-spiking framework for illumination-robust object detection from a single spike stream. SPIRE builds an appearance view from the full packet through detection-oriented illumination adaptation and constructs a sequence of cumulative views from progressively longer prefixes. Each prefix supplies a rate map, an edge map, and a rate difference that tracks changes as spikes accumulate. At selected scales, ANN features and spike-derived initialization jointly set the initial membrane potentials and firing thresholds, allowing semantic cues to shape the responses during temporal encoding. Local spike-count contrasts supervise both branches during training. The residual between measured and ANN-predicted contrasts combines with the spiking features to produce a bounded correction to the fused feature pyramid. On simulated VOC and COCO, SPIRE-D outperforms the strongest baseline by 4.00-5.50 AP50 points across the four under- and overexposure settings. On real PKU-Vidar-DVS recordings, it achieves 67.07 AP50 and 40.10 AP75. SPIRE-D also uses 50.4% fewer MACs than SPFD.

</details>


### [223] [Diffusion Steering via Adaptive Reward Estimation and Resampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34839)
*Abinitha Gourabathina,Sana Tonekaboni,Marzyeh Ghassemi,Akbar Rafiey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练方法TrailMix，利用基础模型自身生成结果的奖励分布来估计引导强度，通过重加权与重采样轨迹提升文生图对齐，并扩展到图像编辑，同时提出SEAM-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图扩散模型虽能响应复杂提示，但常遗漏所要求的目标、属性或关系。作者希望验证：模型能否通过将概率质量重新分配到其更成功的生成结果上，从而更可靠地生成文本对齐的图像。

Method: TrailMix为免训练方法：在去噪过程中由并行轨迹（trails）生成低成本的lookahead图像，用外部评判器对其与提示的对齐度打分；由奖励分数分布估计应以多强力度引导生成达到目标对齐水平，据此对轨迹进行重加权与重采样，偏向更有希望的结果。方法进一步扩展至定向图像编辑，并提出SEAM-Bench（语义编辑与属性保持）基准，评估语义编辑是否保持其余图像内容。

Result: 在三个文生图基准上优于现有基线，在罕见多目标组合上提升尤为明显；在SEAM-Bench与PIE-Bench上验证了受控图像编辑的有效性。

Conclusion: 模型自身可能结果的分布信息可用于指导可靠且可控的生成，为免训练的文生图对齐与编辑提供了新思路。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models can generate images that satisfy complex prompts, yet often produce images that omit requested objects, attributes, or relationships. We ask if models can more reliably generate text-aligned images by redistributing probability toward their more successful outcomes. We propose TrailMix, a training-free method that uses the base model's distribution of possible outcomes to adaptively guide generation toward better text alignment. During denoising, TrailMix generates inexpensive lookahead images from parallel trajectories (trails) and scores their alignment with the prompt using an external judge. The resulting distribution of reward scores allows us to estimate how strongly to steer generation toward a desired alignment level. We use these estimates to reweight and resample trajectories, favoring more promising outcomes. Across three text-to-image benchmarks, TrailMix outperforms existing baselines, with particularly strong gains on rare multi-object compositions. We further extend TrailMix to targeted image editing and introduce SEAM-Bench (Semantic Editing and Attribute Maintenance), a systematic benchmark evaluating whether semantic edits preserve other image content. Experiments on SEAM-Bench and existing benchmark PIE-Bench demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach for controlled image editing. Our results show how a model's own possible outcomes can inform reliable and controllable generation.

</details>


### [224] [Probing Visual Idiosyncrasies in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34843)
*Yongqian Peng,Hongjie Li,Yichao Zhou,Chi Zhang,Yixin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 前沿开源T2I模型生成的图像仍可被高精度溯源：ResNet-50在四个顶级模型间达100%分类准确率，而人类仅62.61%，差异源于低层纹理、物体布局/深度/反照率及风格背景偏好三层视觉特质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前顶级T2I模型输出视觉质量已相当接近，作者想检验输出是否仍携带模型特定特征，这关系到图像溯源、超越聚合质量的模型比较，以及"匿名性"假设下的评测有效性。

Method: 以Text-to-Image Arena排名前四的开源模型（HiDream-O1-Dev、FLUX.2-dev、HunyuanImage-3.0、Qwen-Image-2512）为对象，用标准ResNet-50在未见提示上做来源分类，并开展人类成对判断实验；进一步从低层纹理统计（MLP分类）、物体布局/深度/反照率估计、图像描述三层次归因，并测试严重图像损坏与分布外提示的鲁棒性，最后通过提示线索干预验证因果性。

Result: ResNet-50在留出提示上达100.00%准确率；人类成对任务仅62.61%；仅用低层纹理统计的轻量MLP即可匹敌ResNet-50；布局、深度、反照率分别携带来源信号；字幕显示提示未指定的风格与背景偏好；一句线索提示使人类准确率提升9.03个百分点；结论对分类器选择不敏感，且在严重损坏与OOD提示下依然成立。

Conclusion: T2I模型输出存在稳定、非显而易见的视觉"指纹"，使来源分类可作为度量模型间及真实-生成图像相似性的工具，并警示在人类偏好评测中不应假定模型匿名性。

Abstract: Text-to-Image (T2I) models turn short text descriptions into images, and the leading models now produce images of comparable visual quality. Whether their outputs still carry model-specific characteristics matters for attribution, for model comparison beyond aggregate quality, and for evaluations that assume anonymity. We ask whether images from frontier T2I models can still be classified by source, and which visual properties make this possible. Here we show that a standard ResNet-50 identifies the source of an image among HiDream-O1-Dev, FLUX.2-dev, HunyuanImage-3.0, and Qwen-Image-2512, the four highest-ranked open-source models on the Text-to-Image Arena, with 100.00% accuracy on held-out prompts. Human participants reach only 62.61% on the pairwise version of this task, so the distinguishing cues are not visually obvious. They are also not tied to a particular classifier and survive severe image corruptions and out-of-distribution prompts. We trace them to visual idiosyncrasies at three levels. Low-level texture statistics alone let a lightweight multi-layer perceptron match ResNet-50. Object layout, depth, and albedo estimated from the images each carry a source signal. Image captions reveal model-specific preferences in style and background that the prompts never specify. A one-sentence hint about these cues raises human accuracy by 9.03 percentage points. These idiosyncrasies make source classification a tool for measuring similarity between models and between real and generated images, and they caution against assuming anonymity in human preference evaluation of T2I models.

</details>


### [225] [TIDE-Flow: Fine-Grained Temporal Evidence and Structured Displacement Matching for Spike Optical Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34854)
*Hanwen Liang,Wenxuan Liu,Wenxin Huang,Jingling Yuan,Zhaofei Yu,Xian Zhong,Tiejun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TIDE-Flow脉冲相机光流估计框架，联合利用时序积分证据（TIE）与位移证据编码（DEE），在差分相关循环细化框架中实现高精度运动估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲相机以二值流编码累积光照而非瞬时强度，且时序聚合会削弱细粒度发放模式；同时基于相关性的估计器依赖学习到的匹配特征，缺乏对候选位移空间分布与歧义性的显式刻画。作者旨在解决脉冲数据下的可靠运动估计问题。

Method: TIE模块融合原始脉冲模式、多尺度发放统计、时序矩与边界感知区间线索，在空间特征压缩前保留互补时序信息；DEE模块将结构化候选网格特征与显式位移统计相结合，刻画匹配几何与竞争对应关系；二者嵌入差分相关循环细化框架。

Result: 大量实验验证TIDE-Flow的有效性，受控消融实验证实了时序证据建模与位移证据建模各自的贡献。

Conclusion: 显式的时序积分证据与位移证据编码可提升脉冲光流估计性能，为神经形态视觉中的运动估计提供有效范式。

Abstract: Spike cameras capture high-speed motion through an integrate-and-fire mechanism, providing fine-grained temporal observations without exposure blur. However, their binary streams encode accumulated illumination rather than instantaneous intensity, making reliable motion estimation challenging. Existing spike-based methods exploit temporal aggregation to construct motion representations, but this process may weaken fine-grained firing patterns. Meanwhile, correlation-based estimators often rely on learned matching features without explicitly characterizing the spatial distribution and ambiguity of candidate displacements. To address these challenges, we propose TIDE-Flow, a spike-based optical flow estimation framework that jointly exploits Temporal Integration Evidence (TIE) and Displacement Evidence Encoding (DEE). TIE combines raw spike patterns, multi-scale firing statistics, temporal moments, and boundary-aware interval cues, preserving complementary temporal information before spatial feature compression. DEE further integrates structured candidate-grid features with explicit displacement statistics to characterize matching geometry and competing correspondences. Both components are incorporated into a difference-correlation recurrent refinement framework, enabling effective temporal motion modeling and correspondence estimation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of TIDE-Flow, with controlled ablations validating the contributions of temporal and displacement evidence modeling.

</details>


### [226] [DocDUR-Net: Degradation-Aware Unified Document Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34860)
*Haowei Xu,Peirong Zhang,Xuhan Zheng,Lianwen Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DocDUR-Net，一种退化感知的统一文档图像修复网络，无需外部任务选择，自动从输入图像提取退化引导信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档图像受多种退化影响；单退化方法需按退化类型分别训练部署并在修复前做模型选择；现有统一方法仍依赖外部任务选择，限制自动化。

Method: 退化感知模块DPM提取退化表示并预测退化权重；退化先验引导的自适应特征融合DP-AFF将可学习退化先验与输入提取的退化特征结合；引导信息注入共享修复主干；另支持显式任务选择的手动模式。

Result: 在文档图像修复基准上，统一方法中取得最佳平均PSNR 29.405 dB与SSIM 0.960，并与单退化方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过从输入直接推断退化引导，DocDUR-Net实现了无需外部任务选择的自动统一文档图像修复。

Abstract: Captured document images often suffer from diverse degradations caused by uncontrolled imaging conditions, which reduce readability and obscure fine text structures. Single-degradation restoration methods are usually trained and deployed for individual degradation types, and therefore require model selection before restoration. Unified restoration models alleviate the need to maintain multiple restoration networks by sharing one network across tasks, but representative unified document image restoration methods still depend on external task selection. This dependency limits automatic document image restoration. We propose DocDUR-Net, a degradation-aware unified restoration network that derives restoration guidance directly from the input image. It introduces a Degradation Perception Module (DPM) to extract degradation representations and predict degradation weights, and Degradation Prior-guided Adaptive Feature Fusion (DP-AFF) to combine learnable degradation priors with degradation features extracted from the input. The resulting guidance is injected into a shared restoration backbone, enabling adaptive restoration without external task selection. DocDUR-Net also supports a manual mode with explicit task selection. Experiments on document image restoration benchmarks show that DocDUR-Net achieves the best average PSNR of 29.405 dB and SSIM of 0.960 among unified methods while remaining competitive with single-degradation methods.

</details>


### [227] [BOP-Refer: Benchmark for Monocular 3D and 2D Object Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34862)
*Tomas Hodan,Vineet Bhat,Martin Sundermeyer,Jonathan Tremblay,Yash Patel,Médéric Fourmy,Stephen Tyree,Junwen Huang,Varun Burde,Martin Cífka,Eric Brachmann,Vincent Lepetit,Stan Birchfield,Jiri Matas,Carsten Rother*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个面向日常与工业物体的单目3D/2D指代定位基准BOP-Refer，系统评测17个前沿VLM，发现2D表现较好（最优72.6% AP_IoU2D）但3D极差（12.4% AP_IoU3D），主要误差源于度量尺度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLM）在2D指代定位上进展显著，但缺乏针对真实场景日常与工业物体的单目3D指代定位评测基准，难以衡量模型在度量尺度上的能力。论文希望填补这一空白并诊断3D定位短板。

Method: 构建BOP-Refer基准：从9个BOP数据集中采样图像，配以VLM生成并经人工校验的指代表达；利用物体3D模型与6DoF位姿标注推导精确的3D与2D真值包围框。随后对Gemini、GPT、Claude、Qwen、Gemma等主流闭源与开源家族的17个前沿VLM进行系统评测。

Result: 最佳模型Gemini 4 Carbon在2D取得72.6% AP_IoU2D，而3D仅12.4% AP_IoU3D，尽管3D的IoU阈值更宽松。预测的3D框通常在图像中居中于目标物体，但深度方向偏差常超过物体自身尺寸，表明度量尺度估计是当前最主要的误差来源。

Conclusion: VLM的单目3D指代定位能力远落后于2D，瓶颈在于依赖可靠物体尺寸先验的度量尺度估计；BOP-Refer为后续研究提供了标准化评测平台与明确的改进方向。

Abstract: We introduce BOP-Refer, the first benchmark for monocular 3D and 2D grounding of everyday and industrial objects sourced from 9 diverse BOP datasets. We pair images from these datasets with VLM-generated and human-verified referring expressions, and derive accurate ground-truth 3D and 2D bounding boxes of the referred objects from their 3D models and 6DoF pose annotations. Our systematic evaluation of 17 frontier VLMs across the major proprietary and open-weight families (Gemini, GPT, Claude, Qwen, Gemma) shows that the best model, Gemini 4 Carbon, reaches 72.6% AP_IoU2D in 2D but only 12.4% AP_IoU3D in 3D, despite the more lenient IoU thresholds in 3D. The predicted 3D boxes are usually centered on the target objects in the image but often off in depth by more than the object's size, suggesting that metric scale estimation, which needs reliable object size priors, is currently by far the main source of error.

</details>


### [228] [Focus Only on What's Important: Training-Free Focus Maps for Frozen Vision Transformers in Long-Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34863)
*Harsha Karipineni,Rajesh Reddy Chintha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 为冻结的 DINOv3 ViT + 训练好的 Cutie 头引入无需训练的"聚焦图"：在分割器预期目标的区域保持全分辨率，其余区域进行更粗的 token merging，以降低视频目标分割的编码器计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT 作为视频目标分割的编码器性能强但代价高；现有免训练 token merging 在整幅图像上均匀剪枝，连被跟踪目标也一并被合并，造成精度损失。

Method: 保护区域取速度平移掩码与块匹配掩码的并集；执行相机补偿的"surprise"（意外度）检验；将不确定区域交由对照合并器处理；当所有目标丢失时回退到该对照合并器。仅在 LVOS V2 valid 上按预先设定协议、三个 ViT-S 随机种子评测，并附 DAVIS 2017 val 与对照实验。

Result: LVOS V2 valid 上 ViT-S 较 ALGM 0.98 提升 +2.76 J&F（95% 目标级 CI [+1.22,+4.44]，视频簇 CI [+0.99,+4.52]），计入的编码器 FLOPs 少 16%（0.385 vs 0.459），距稠密编码器基线差 1.0 J&F；ViT-B 上较 ALGM 0.95 提升 +3.89 [+2.03,+5.87]。DAVIS 2017 val 上与稠密编码器差距在 -0.19 J&F（ViT-S）/-0.12（ViT-B）。对照显示仅保护上一帧掩码在更高 FLOPs（0.486）下精度相当或更高，说明期望与意外度机制主要用于减少计入的编码器计算。实际延迟：12 视频 LVOS 样本上启动含时均值未降，稳态仅低 3%–8%，同等工程优化的 ALGM 在 ViT-S 与 ViT-B 上更快。

Conclusion: 该方法以更少的计入编码器 FLOPs 取得优于 ALGM 的 J&F，但计算量下降未转化为端到端延迟收益；且简单掩码保护对照已可匹敌精度，表明收益主要来自计算计量层面而非精度层面的本质改进。第 5.2 节另行讨论 Cutie 永久记忆调度的延迟与内存权衡。

Abstract: Vision transformers are strong but costly image encoders for video object segmentation. Training-free token merging cuts compute uniformly across the frame, including on tracked objects. We give a frozen DINOv3 ViT inside a trained Cutie head a training-free focus map: full resolution where the segmenter expects its objects and coarser merging elsewhere. Protecting only the previous frame's mask works on short clips but fails on long videos, where fast motion pushes targets outside the margin and into background merging, locking in loss. Our policy instead takes the union of velocity-shifted and block-matched masks, runs a camera-compensated surprise check, delegates uncertain regions to the compared merger, and falls back to it when all objects are lost. On long videos (LVOS V2 valid, pre-specified protocol, three ViT-S seeds), it beats ALGM 0.98 by +2.76 J&F (95% object CI [+1.22,+4.44]; video-cluster [+0.99,+4.52]) at 16% fewer counted encoder FLOPs (0.385 vs. 0.459), sitting 1.0 J&F point below the dense-encoder baseline; at ViT-B the gain over ALGM 0.95 is +3.89 [+2.03,+5.87]. On DAVIS 2017 val it stays within -0.19 J&F of the dense encoder at ViT-S (-0.12 at ViT-B). Controls qualify the result: protecting only the previous mask under ALGM matches or exceeds accuracy at higher FLOPs (0.486), so expectation and surprise mainly reduce counted encoder compute. Practically, lower counted encoder compute did not reduce startup-inclusive mean Cutie step time on a 12-video LVOS sample (3%-8% lower in steady state; an equally engineered ALGM is faster at ViT-S and ViT-B). Section 5.2 separately examines a Cutie permanent-memory schedule and its latency and memory trade-offs. Code: https://github.com/boletoms/focused-cv

</details>


### [229] [Towards Closing the Performance Gap in Cross-Modal Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34877)
*Ofer Idan,Dan Badur,Yoli Shavit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CrossPlace：两阶段跨模态地点识别框架，通过提高视觉token密度、增强文本编码器能力与局部跨模态匹配，大幅缩小与单模态检索/视觉地点识别的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨模态地点识别（CMPR）可用语言描述匹配地理标记参考图像库，实现无视觉查询定位，对语言引导导航有重要价值。但现有视觉-语言模型即使经过任务适配也远落后于视觉地点识别（VPR）基线。与通用文本-图像检索不同，地点识别依赖细微局部线索区分视觉相似地点，因此作者假设主要瓶颈在于图像区域与文本细节的细粒度对应建模，需要更高的视觉token粒度、更强的文本表征能力和局部跨模态匹配。

Method: 提出两阶段CMPR框架CrossPlace。第一阶段进行全局检索：采用联合对齐的DINOv2-B/14视觉主干，搭配渐进式微调的BGE-Large文本编码器。第二阶段用轻量级token级交叉注意力重排器对Top候选进行精化。并通过系统性分析隔离影响检索精度的关键驱动因素。

Result: 分析表明视觉token密度带来最大性能提升，文本编码器容量与任务微调提供互补增益。同时将CMPR评测扩展到城市尺度及恶劣天气与光照条件。在全部现有及新增基准上，CrossPlace相较以往跨模态方法取得两位数Recall@1提升，缩小了与单模态文本检索的差距，并将与单模态VPR的差距进一步缩小。

Conclusion: 细粒度跨模态匹配（尤其是视觉token密度）是CMPR的关键瓶颈，CrossPlace的两阶段设计与相应组件有效验证了这一假设，并在多种真实场景下显著优于先前跨模态方法，代码与基准已开源。

Abstract: Cross-modal place recognition (CMPR) matches natural language descriptions against databases of geo-tagged reference images, enabling localization without visual queries. Despite its potential for language-guided navigation, existing vision-language models lag far behind visual place recognition (VPR) baselines, even after task adaptation. Unlike general-purpose text-to-image retrieval, place recognition relies on subtle local cues to distinguish visually similar locations. We therefore hypothesize that a primary bottleneck in CMPR is capturing fine-grained correspondences between image regions and text details, which requires higher visual token granularity, strong text representation capacity, and local cross-modal matching. To address this challenge, we propose CrossPlace, a two-stage CMPR framework. Stage 1 performs global retrieval by pairing a jointly aligned DINOv2-B/14 visual backbone with a progressively fine-tuned BGE-Large text encoder, while Stage 2 refines top candidates using a lightweight token-level cross-attention reranker. Through systematic analysis, we isolate the drivers of retrieval accuracy and show that visual token density provides the largest performance improvement, while text encoder capacity and task fine-tuning offer complementary gains. To evaluate robustness under realistic conditions, we also extend CMPR evaluation to city-scale and severe weather and illumination settings. Across all existing and new benchmarks, CrossPlace achieves double-digit Recall@1 improvements over prior cross-modal methods, closes the gap to unimodal text retrieval, and narrows it to unimodal VPR. Code and benchmarks are available at https://github.com/oferidan1/CrossPlace

</details>


### [230] [Stranger Things: When Objects Appear Without Their Typical Neighbors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34881)
*Siddhartha Gairola,Jiahao Xie,Anna Kukleva,Francesco Locatello,Bernt Schiele*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统诊断现代视觉模型中的"典型性偏置"（typicality bias），并发布 STRANGE-Bench 基准与 Anchor-Atypicality Sampling（AAS）数据增强方法来缓解该偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉上下文是物体识别的重要线索，但模型过度依赖上下文、依据典型邻居而非物体自身视觉特征进行预测，形成典型性偏置。该问题在 Transformer 之前被广泛研究，但业界普遍假定架构与规模提升会自然解决，缺乏跨现代视觉技术栈的系统诊断。

Method: 1) 用物体对上的归一化点互信息（NPMI）量化典型性偏置；2) 构建 STRANGE-Bench：逼真、布局可控的合成基准，覆盖 17 个从典型到从未共现的物体对子层级；3) 评测五类模型家族（专用检测器、开放词汇检测器、自监督、视觉-语言、多模态大模型）与三项识别任务；4) 提出 AAS——基于 NPMI 信号的粘贴式增强采样规则，将训练期共现分布向非典型伙伴倾斜。

Result: 偏置普遍存在：当物体脱离典型共现环境时，性能下降 5–11 个百分点，且并不随规模稳定缩小。AAS 在 COCO 自然图像切分上将典型性差距最多降低 1.6 p.p.，在合成 STRANGE-Bench 上降低 23.0 p.p.，覆盖三个模型家族，且不损害整体性能。

Conclusion: 典型性偏置是现代视觉技术栈中持续存在的失败模式，无法靠架构与规模自然消解；通过基于共现统计的信号驱动数据增强（AAS）可在不牺牲总体性能的前提下显著缓解该偏置，并提供可复用的诊断框架与基准。

Abstract: Visual context is a powerful cue for object recognition: scenes inform what, where, and at what scale objects are likely to appear. Yet over-reliance on context becomes a failure mode when models predict objects based on their typical neighbors rather than the objects' own visual features, which we call typicality bias. While extensively studied in the pre-Transformer era, it has often been assumed that architectural and scale improvements would naturally solve it, and a systematic diagnostic across the modern vision stack remains absent. We provide such a diagnostic framework. We measure typicality bias via normalized pointwise mutual information (NPMI) over object pairs, and introduce STRANGE-Bench (Synthetic Typicality-RANked Grounded Evaluation Benchmark): a photorealistic, layout-controlled benchmark spanning 17 sub-tiers from typical to never-co-occurring object pairs. Across five model families (specialist detectors, open-vocabulary detectors, self-supervised, vision-language, and multimodal large language models) and three recognition tasks (supervised detection, open-vocabulary detection, and classification), the bias is prevalent: gaps of 5-11 p.p. persist when objects appear outside their typical company, and do not consistently shrink with scale. To mitigate it, we turn to data augmentation: existing augmentations are designed for visual diversity, not for breaking co-occurrence shortcuts, and sample from the very distribution that produces typicality bias. We propose Anchor-Atypicality Sampling (AAS), a simple sampling rule for paste-based augmentations that uses the NPMI signal to tilt the training-time co-occurrence distribution toward atypical partners. AAS reduces the typicality gap by up to 1.6 p.p. on natural-image COCO splits and 23.0 p.p. on synthetic STRANGE-Bench across three model families, without sacrificing overall performance.

</details>


### [231] [What a Drop Reveals: Sim-to-Real Material Estimation for Deformable Objects from Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34894)
*Ziyue Peng,Yanxi Hu,Junyoung Kim,Andrew Chen,Jin Yang,Chandrajit L. Bajaj,Etienne Vouga,William T. Freeman,Qixing Huang,Brandon Y. Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhysReader：仅用仿真训练的预测器，从单段真实坠落视频中估计形变物体的密度归一化刚度E/ρ与屈服应力σ_Y/ρ，并揭示"仿真中最优输入≠真实域中最优输入"的域间隙现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单段真实坠落视频反推材料属性，但真实视频没有材料标签，预测器只能在合成数据上开发与验证；因此需要检验"仿真验证所选的输入表征"能否迁移到真实视频。

Method: 前馈预测器仅在仿真中训练；对比不同几何输入（3D形变轨迹、带深度估计的3D提升轨迹、2D光流轨迹、模拟器深度、LiDAR深度、渲染回放精确深度）；PhysReader以视频+2D短光流轨迹为输入，在仿真中通过匹配"可见精确3D形变"的教师模型学习3D提升；构建PhysReader-Real基准：16个日常物体各坠落5次，参考材料参数由同一物体多次坠落联合拟合并与拉伸试验校验。

Result: 合成验证对几何输入排序错误：3D提升轨迹在仿真中最准，但在真实坠落中最差，且更精确的深度（模拟器深度、LiDAR、渲染精确深度）也无法修复；2D光流轨迹在仿真中无增益却在真实域迁移最佳。PhysReader在PhysReader-Real上比所有其他输入与基线更接近参考材料，仅略逊于以其预测为初值的逐场景优化器（每段视频49分钟 vs 12秒）。

Conclusion: 材料属性应从两个域中感知系统都能一致测量的量（2D轨迹）中读取，而非仿真中最具信息量的量（3D形变）；仿真验证不足以评估跨域迁移，需真实基准如PhysReader-Real，且PhysReader在精度-效率权衡上具优势。

Abstract: How an object deforms when it hits the ground reveals what it is made of. From a single real video of a drop, we estimate a deformable object's density-normalized stiffness $E/ρ$, and its yield stress $σ_Y/ρ$ when the impact causes plastic deformation, with a feed-forward predictor trained only in simulation. Real videos carry no material labels, so such predictors are developed and validated on synthetic data alone. We show that synthetic validation ranks their geometric inputs in the wrong order. Material acts through 3D deformation, and tracklets lifted to 3D with estimated depth are indeed the most accurate input in simulation. However, on real drops they are the least accurate, and better depth does not repair them: the failure persists with simulator depth in training, LiDAR depth at test time, and exact depth on rendered replays of the real drops. Short 2D optical-flow tracklets do the opposite: they improve nothing in simulation, yet they transfer best. Material is therefore better read from what perception measures consistently in both domains than from what is most informative in simulation. Our predictor, PhysReader, reads the video and 2D tracklets and learns the lift to 3D in simulation by matching a teacher that sees exact 3D deformation. To measure transfer, we introduce PhysReader-Real: 16 everyday objects dropped five times each, with reference materials fit jointly to each object's drops and checked against tensile tests. On PhysReader-Real, PhysReader is closer to the reference materials than every other input and baseline. The one exception is a per-scene optimizer started from PhysReader's prediction, which ends slightly closer but takes 49 minutes per video instead of 12 seconds.

</details>


### [232] [Enhancing Maritime Domain Awareness using Latent Space Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34899)
*Rikard Vinge,Miguel A. Belenguer-Plomer,Isabelle Wittmann,Octavian Dumitru,Michele Lazzarini,Sergio Albani,Conrad M Albrecht*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作基于地理空间基础模型 TerraMind，直接利用 Sentinel-1 SAR 图像的压缩潜空间嵌入（而非原始影像）进行船舶检测、定位与类型识别，以缓解数据引力瓶颈，并发布约 2000 幅 Sentinel-1 图像与时间对齐 AIS 信号配套的海事遥感数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运营级对地观测处理日益受"数据引力"瓶颈制约——原始大体积影像的传输与存储成本高。作者希望验证地理空间基础模型（GeoFM）的压缩嵌入能否在潜空间直接支撑海事态势感知（MDA）任务，契合 Embed2Scale 项目目标。

Method: 以 TerraMind 为骨干，对 Sentinel-1 GRD 影像的压缩潜表示进行线性与非线性探针（probing），完成船舶检测、定位与 AIS 类型码分类；同时构建约 2000 幅 Sentinel-1 影像与时间对齐商业船舶 AIS 信号配对数据集，经人工核验边界框质量及 AIS 报告船长与观测船体尺度的一致性，并交叉核对公开船舶记录与照片，最终整理为适配 NeuCo-Bench 神经压缩基准框架的格式并公开于 HuggingFace。

Result: 船舶检测 ROC-AUC 达 0.95；对长度超过 100 m 的船舶，定位中位误差约 300 m；AIS 类型码分类在多数类上准确率 62%，且该结果与船长高度相关。

Conclusion: 压缩嵌入可有效支撑粗粒度检测与定位，但在细粒度 MDA 理解任务上仍受限，结果同时揭示了 GeoFM 在该场景下的潜力与当前局限；发布的数据集为该方向的基准评测提供支持。

Abstract: This work presents a novel application of Earth Observation (EO) embeddings for Maritime Domain Awareness (MDA) from the perspective of the geospatial foundation model TerraMind. By operating directly on compressed latent-space representations rather than on the original Sentinel-1 imagery, this approach directly addresses the data gravity bottleneck that increasingly constrains operational EO processing, in line with the objectives of the Embed2Scale project. In particular, linear and non-linear probing of compressed embeddings, latent space representations of synthetic aperture radar (SAR) imagery, is used to detect, localise, and characterise vessels from Sentinel-1 GRD images. Vessel detection achieves a ROC-AUC of 0.95, while vessel localisation has a median error of around 300 m for vessels longer than 100 m, and vessel AIS type-code classification achieves 62% accuracy for the majority classes. The latter, however, is highly correlated with vessel length. These results reveal both the potential and current limitations of Geospatial Foundation Models (GeoFMs) when applied to fine-grained MDA understanding in real-world scenarios.
  Additionally, a new maritime remote sensing dataset is released, pairing about 2,000 Sentinel-1 images with temporally aligned AIS signals from commercial vessels. Through manual inspection, each candidate sample was verified for bounding-box quality and for consistency between the AIS-reported vessel length and the observed vessel extent, cross-checking vessel identity and location against publicly available vessel records and, where available, photographs. The dataset was made ready for use with the NeuCo-Bench neural compression benchmark framework, publicly available on HuggingFace through https://huggingface.co/datasets/embed2scale/S1-single-ship-downstream .

</details>


### [233] [ProbFaS: Probabilistic Video Frame Selection for MLLMs via Multi-Fidelity Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34903)
*Tianru Zhang,Mayank Nautiyal,Hugo Eidmann,Prashant Singh,Ekta Vats*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProbFaS：无训练、无标签的概率式关键帧选择框架，用多保真高斯过程（co-kriging）融合廉价的图文对齐分数与稀疏的MLLM内部yes/no判断，再以时序行列式点过程选帧，在14种免训练选择器中精度最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频+多模态大模型（MLLM）推理代价过高，帧选择至关重要；均匀采样会漏掉任务相关关键帧，而现有免训练选择器依赖的图文对齐（ITA）排序与下游准确率相关性弱，甚至在超过40%的问题上呈负相关，说明ITA信号与MLLM真实需求不匹配。

Method: 将稠密ITA分数视为廉价但有偏的观测、稀疏的MLLM yes/no判断视为准确但昂贵的观测，通过co-kriging（多保真高斯过程）在全部帧上校准相关性；再以时序行列式点过程（temporal DPP）选取既覆盖相关性峰值又保持时间覆盖的帧。全流程无需训练与标签。

Result: 在Video-MME、MLVU、LongVideoBench上，于15种设置中的13种（8–128帧）优于14种SOTA免训练选择器与均匀采样，最高超过最强基线3.1个百分点；在MLVU和LongVideoBench上，32帧即可匹敌或超过128帧的顶级基线，以及最多768帧的均匀采样。

Conclusion: ITA信号并非可靠的相关性代理，融合MLLM内部判断进行多保真校准能显著提升关键帧选择质量，且可在极低帧预算下达到甚至超越高帧预算基线，验证了概率式融合+多样性选帧的有效性。

Abstract: Processing long videos with multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is prohibitively expensive, making informative frame selection essential for accurate video understanding. The default uniform sampling often misses task-relevant keyframes, while recent training-free selectors instead pick frames by how well each one matches the question under an image-text alignment (ITA) score. However, we show that ITA signals correspond poorly with the MLLM's true needs: their rankings correlate weakly with downstream accuracy and are even negatively correlated on over 40% of questions, whereas the MLLM's internal yes/no judgment yields a much stronger signal. We present ProbFaS, a training- and label-free probabilistic pipeline that fuses both signals for key frame selection. ProbFaS treats dense ITA scores as cheap, biased measurements and sparse MLLM judgments as accurate, costly ones, unifying them via co-kriging, a multi-fidelity Gaussian process, to calibrate relevance across all frames. A temporal determinantal point process then selects frames capturing relevance peaks while preserving temporal coverage. Across Video-MME, MLVU and LongVideoBench, ProbFaS is the most accurate among fourteen state-of-the-art training-free selectors and uniform sampling in 13 of 15 settings (8-128 frames), outperforming the strongest baseline by up to 3.1 percentage points. On MLVU and LongVideoBench, 32 ProbFaS frames match or surpass the top baseline at 128 frames and uniform sampling with up to 768 frames.

</details>


### [234] [Agentic LongLive: Asynchronous Agentic Memory for Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34907)
*Shaoteng Liu,Shuai Yang,Wei Huang,Luozhou Wang,Song Han,Yukang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Agentic LongLive：一种异步智能体记忆机制，用独立 GPU 上的记忆智能体维护人/物/景的文本状态与演化图像，在不阻塞实时生成的前提下显著提升长视频生成的长程一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时自回归视频生成器可流式生成数分钟视频并即时接受新提示，但难以在长交互叙事中保持人物、物体与场景一致。现有离线多镜头生成的智能体方案需预先读取完整脚本、逐镜头规划并重生成失败镜头，与直播流式场景不兼容。

Method: 记忆智能体运行于独立 GPU，仅读取已揭示的提示、不读取生成视频，为每个实体维护文本状态与演化图像；由实体记忆合成全局图像与分镜记忆图像，经辅助 GPU 预填充到生成器的 attention sink；为命名实体的提示写入 entity card。用小语言模型经强化学习训练，配合以下游视频结果校准的图像奖励，在未知下一提示前决定记忆内容。评测引入 LongStory-Online 基准（25 个故事、857 个镜头，逐镜头揭示）。

Result: 相比基础生成器 LongLive-2.0，持久状态准确率 +26.3、主角身份一致性 +44.9、重访场景一致性 +40.4，额外生成时间约 1%（H100）。单台 8×B200 节点、BF16 生成器下达 34.3 FPS，快于实时；14 分钟故事中图像质量稳定；在两个 MiniMax-H3 生成器上提升身份一致性，其中 LongLive-H3 亦可与之同步实时运行。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首个由 LLM 智能体维护文本与图像记忆的实时视频生成器，实现了"视频不等记忆、错误不回写"的解耦式异步长程一致性方案。

Abstract: Real-time autoregressive video generators stream minutes of video and accept new prompts on the fly, but struggle to keep people, objects, and places consistent over a long interactive story. Agent systems provide such memory for offline multi-shot generation, yet they read the whole script in advance, plan each shot before generating it, and regenerate failed shots, which a live stream does not allow. We present Agentic LongLive, an asynchronous agentic memory for long video generation. A memory agent on separate GPUs reads only the prompts revealed so far, never the generated video, and keeps a text state and evolving images for every person, object, and scene. From this entity memory it composes a global image and a per-shot memory image, which an auxiliary GPU prefills into the generator's attention sink before the next shot starts, and writes entity cards for the prompts that name them, so the video never waits for memory and video errors are not written back. A small language model, trained with reinforcement learning and an image reward calibrated using downstream video outcomes, decides what to remember before the next prompt is known. On LongStory-Online, a new benchmark of 25 stories (857 shots) revealed one shot at a time, Agentic LongLive improves persistent-state accuracy of LongLive-2.0, its base generator, by 26.3 points, protagonist identity by 44.9, and revisited-place consistency by 40.4, at about 1% extra generation time on H100. The full system streams faster than real time on one 8xB200 node with the generator in BF16 (34.3 FPS), keeps image quality stable over 14-minute stories, and improves identity on two MiniMax-H3 generators, one of which, LongLive-H3, also runs in real time with it. To our knowledge, it is the first real-time video generator with text and image memory kept by an LLM agent.

</details>


### [235] [LiveVAE: Causal Window Attention Makes Efficient Streaming Video VAE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34912)
*Jiachen An,Sijie Zhao,Le Li,Hongyu Liu,Zhiyuan Li,Tianyu Yang,Xiaoyu Kong,Xunliang Cai,Yong Zhang,Chi-Man Pun,Xiaoming Wei,Xiaodong Cun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出现有视频VAE的编解码严重冗余，实际只需局部时空建模，据此提出基于因果窗口注意力的流式编解码器LiveVAE，在保持预训练潜空间的前提下实现显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型虽已趋于快速、可流式，但视频VAE仍是交互式生成与编辑的主要延迟瓶颈；现有VAE及其加速方法多依赖重型卷积或全帧注意力，且大多只加速解码，编码在流式条件生成与编辑中仍耗时。作者由此质疑：视频VAE的编码与解码是否真的需要全局时空建模。

Method: 通过分析预训练视频VAE的有效感受野，发现编码和解码单个patch几乎仅依赖其空间与时间邻域，即具有强局部性。基于此设计LiveVAE：使用因果窗口注意力的流式编码器与解码器，降低单帧计算量并将时间KV缓存保持有界；训练上采用编码器/解码器解耦蒸馏后进行联合微调，以保持原有潜空间，使其可作为原VAE的直接替代。

Result: 在MiniMax-H3上，LiveVAE相较官方VAE实现5.2–6.2倍编码加速与3.0–3.3倍解码加速，且PSNR更高；配合专用推理基础设施，加速比提升至13.4倍且几乎无质量损失。

Conclusion: 视频VAE的全局时空建模并necessary，局部因果窗口注意力即可在保持潜空间与生成质量的同时大幅降低编解码延迟，为流式视频生成与编辑提供了可插拔的高效VAE方案。

Abstract: While video diffusion models have grown faster and increasingly streamable, the video VAE remains a primary latency bottleneck across interactive generation and editing pipelines. Existing VAEs and their acceleration methods rely mainly on heavy convolutions or full frame attention, and most focus only on decoding, leaving encoding costly for streaming conditional generation and editing. This raises a natural question: Do video VAE encoding and decoding actually require such global spatiotemporal modeling? By analyzing the effective receptive fields of pretrained video VAEs, we find that both encoding and decoding a patch rely almost entirely on its spatial and temporal neighbors. To exploit this strong locality, we introduce LiveVAE, a streaming encoder and decoder built on causal window attention, which cuts per frame compute while keeping the temporal KV cache bounded for streaming. Decoupled encoder/decoder distillation followed by joint finetuning preserves the pretrained latent space, making LiveVAE a drop-in replacement for the original VAE. On MiniMax-H3, LiveVAE achieves 5.2-6.2x faster encoding and 3.0-3.3x faster decoding than the official VAE with higher PSNR. Paired with our dedicated inference infrastructure, LiveVAE pushes the speedup to 13.4x with almost no quality loss. Code and models will be released.

</details>


### [236] [High-Fidelity Image Matting with Pixel-Space Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34929)
*Baihan Yang,Xin Lin,Yufan Wei,Baiang Li,Yuheng Liu,Juanxi Tian,Xiaohui Xie,Truong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PixelMatte，将预训练像素空间扩散 Transformer 适配到高保真图像抠图，无需额外条件编码器，单次前向即可预测 alpha，并在多基准上显著降低 SAD 与梯度误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的抠图方法多依赖潜空间扩散模型，其压缩表示与 VAE 重建会与抠图所需的高频精度产生失配，限制了细节与边界保真度。

Method: 复用骨干网络原生的 patch embedding，将输入图像、trimap 与含噪 alpha 作为空间对齐的 token 联合处理，实现纯像素空间的条件预测，无需额外条件编码器或适配器；仅从纯文生图模型迁移即可获得图像条件抠图能力；配合像素级精确的合成监督与真实图像微调；并引入 LayerShift 与 High-Pass Refiner 两个轻量校准模块，分别提升区域不透明度准确性与边界保真度。

Result: 在四个基准与两个不同的像素扩散骨干上验证；相比 RenderMatte 在 P3M-500-NP 与 AM-2K 上 SAD 与梯度误差降低 24–44%，在 AIM-500 与 RenderMatte-2K 上表现具竞争力。

Conclusion: 像素空间生成先验对精度敏感的图像抠图具有显著有效性，可作为替代潜空间扩散的高保真范式。

Abstract: Diffusion models have shown strong potential for image matting by transferring generative priors learned from large-scale pretraining. However, existing approaches largely rely on latent diffusion models, whose compressed representations and VAE-based reconstruction introduce a mismatch with the high-frequency precision required for matting. We present PixelMatte, a framework that adapts pretrained pixel-space diffusion transformers for high-fidelity image matting. We show that strong image-conditioned matting capabilities can be acquired even from a pure text-to-image model without paired image-to-image pretraining. Our approach reuses the backbone's native patch embedding to jointly process the input image, trimap, and noisy alpha matte as spatially aligned tokens, enabling direct pixel-space prediction without additional condition encoders or adapters. With pixel-accurate synthetic supervision and real-image fine-tuning, PixelMatte predicts alpha mattes in a single forward pass. Two lightweight calibration modules, LayerShift and High-Pass Refiner, further improve regional opacity accuracy and boundary fidelity. Experiments across four benchmarks and two distinct pixel diffusion backbones demonstrate substantial improvements over existing methods, reducing SAD and gradient errors by 24-44% relative to RenderMatte on P3M-500-NP and AM-2K, while achieving competitive performance on AIM-500 and RenderMatte-2K. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of pixel-space generative priors for precision-sensitive image matting. Project page: https://white-yc.github.io/pixelmatte/.

</details>


### [237] [DynMF2: Rigid Motion Factorization by Expectation-Maximization for Monocular 4D Reconstruction and Motion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34931)
*Agelos Kratimenos,Yile Hua,Kostas Daniilidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DynMF2将场景运动建模为少量SE(3)刚体轨迹，每个点只跟随其中一条，无需掩码，直接由3D点轨迹通过EM闭式求解运动分解，实现单目动态场景重建与分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目随意拍摄的视频对动态场景约束不足：某点仅从每个视角被观测一次，它沿相机轴向滑动仍能匹配所有帧，导致重建欠定。因此需要引入运动本身的结构作为先验约束。

Method: 基于DynMF，将场景运动表示为若干条SE(3)刚体轨迹，每个点严格隶属于单一运动、不进行混合。直接在3D点轨迹上以EM算法求解分解：M步以闭式解为每个运动拟合刚体变换，E步将每个点重分配给误差最小的运动；相机运动作为背景的一条额外运动，故无需掩码。再用三个工具修正分解：requery在跟踪器漏检处补充轨迹，merge合并共同运动的运动，split拆分不一致的运动，使运动数量自适应场景。

Result: 无掩码条件下，在iPhone与NVIDIA数据集的新视角合成上明显优于基于掩码的基线，在iPhone的3D跟踪上亦更优；能恢复掩码遗漏的细薄部件、细节及远处背景中的小运动物体；在MOVi-F与iTACO场景的运动分割上具竞争力，尽管从未针对分割训练。

Conclusion: 场景中"什么在运动"并非预先规定，而是由刚体运动分解自身浮现出来；运动结构作为约束可有效消解单目动态重建的欠定性。

Abstract: A single casual video leaves a moving scene under-determined: a point seen once from each place can slide toward or away from the camera and still match every frame. We constrain the reconstruction with the structure of the motion itself. Building on DynMF, we model a scene's motion as a handful of SE(3) trajectories, each rigid, and let every point follow one of them alone, never a blend. DynMF2 computes this factorization directly from 3D point trajectories, with no rendering, by Expectation-Maximization (EM). Its M-step fits, in closed form, every rigid motion to the points assigned to it, and its E-step reassigns every point to the motion that explains it with the least error. The background is simply one more motion, the camera's, so no masks are needed. Three tools then revise the factorization: requery adds trajectories where the rendering shows parts the tracker missed, merge joins motions that move together, and split separates those that do not. Thus the number of motions follows the scene. Without masks, DynMF2 clearly outperforms the mask-based baselines in view synthesis on iPhone and NVIDIA and in 3D tracking on iPhone. It recovers what masks leave out, from thin parts and details to small movers far in the background. Its motions segment the moving parts of MOVi-F and iTACO scenes competitively, although DynMF2 is never trained to segment. What moves in a scene is not decided in advance: it emerges from the rigid motions themselves.

</details>


### [238] [A Mesh for Every Moment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34939)
*Roy Or-El,Jeong Joon Park,Ira Kemelmacher-Shlizerman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MEMO：用高斯过程式的每高斯运动模型重建多相机动态场景几何，逐帧提取网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态高斯泼溅方法要么仅建模外观，要么只针对前景掩码分割出的孤立物体，且均依赖TSDF融合或泊松重建，固定网格分辨率限制了细节；同时大而快的运动常被忽视。

Method: 每高斯位置与旋转随时间表示为时间高斯核的组合（受高斯过程启发的运动模型），使高斯在整段序列中保持为有效表面元并隐式携带动态几何，从而可在任意时刻由高斯定义场导出网格；并提出基于点轨迹的初始化，闭式拟合每个高斯的初始轨迹以适应大快速运动。

Result: 在新基准Blender-Moments（9个动画场景、逐帧GT网格）及两个真实数据集上，几何重建精度优于SOTA，新视角合成与训练时间具竞争力。

Conclusion: MEMO实现完整动态场景的逐帧高质量网格重建，兼顾几何精度与效率。

Abstract: We present MEMO (a Mesh for Every MOment), a method that reconstructs the geometry of complete dynamic scenes, captured by multiple synchronized cameras, and extracts a mesh at every frame. Existing dynamic Gaussian splatting methods either model appearance alone or target isolated objects segmented by foreground masks, and all of them recover meshes by TSDF fusion or Poisson reconstruction, whose fixed grids limit the detail they can capture. At the core of MEMO is a per-Gaussian motion model inspired by Gaussian processes, which represents each Gaussian's position and rotation over time as a combination of temporal Gaussian kernels. The model keeps every Gaussian a valid surface element throughout the sequence and carries an implicit representation of the dynamic geometry, so a mesh can be derived at any time from a field defined by the Gaussians. Although common in real captures, large and fast motion poses a challenge that dynamic Gaussian splatting methods usually overlook. To account for it, we design a track-based initialization scheme that fits each Gaussian's initial trajectory to point tracks in closed form. We evaluate MEMO on Blender-Moments, a new benchmark of nine animated scenes with ground-truth meshes at every frame, and on two real-world datasets. MEMO reconstructs the most accurate geometry among state-of-the-art methods, and remains competitive in novel-view synthesis and training time. Project page: https://memo-4d.github.io

</details>


### [239] [LuxAvatar: Real-Time Relightable Head Avatars via Causal Neural Shading of 3D Intrinsic Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34940)
*Yipengjing Sun,Chenyang Wang,Boyao Zhou,Jiapeng Zhu,Ka Leong Cheng,Shengping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LuxAvatar：单张人像驱动的实时、可重光照3D头部化身动画前馈框架，通过可动画3D内在高斯代理 + 因果自回归神经着色器分解，并用异构蒸馏实现两步实时流式渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以同时兼顾3D可控性与复杂面部光传输：显式PBR渲染难建模真实光照效果，扩散式重光照方法推理慢、无法实时。作者希望在单张人像输入下实现兼具3D控制、真实重光照与实时流式性能的头部化身。

Method: 将化身合成分解为两部分：(1) 可动画的3D内在高斯代理，解耦规范几何与反射率属性，将形变高斯快速光栅化为视角与运动一致的PBR缓冲；(2) 以PBR缓冲为条件的因果自回归神经着色器，把前向渲染建模为条件视频扩散过程，绕过显式PBR计算。采用异构蒸馏，将预训练多步双向重光照教师模型蒸馏为两步因果自回归学生模型以降低条件与解码延迟；并构建混合重光照数据集进行领域微调。

Result: 实现3D可控的实时人像动画与重光照，流式推理实时运行；在视觉保真度、运动控制与光照准确性上优于现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 内在高斯代理与因果神经着色器的解耦式设计，配合异构蒸馏与领域数据微调，可在单图输入下统一实现实时性、3D控制与物理真实的重光照，为流式头部化身提供可行范式。

Abstract: We present LuxAvatar, a feed-forward framework for real-time head avatar animation and relighting from a single portrait. To achieve both 3D controllability and complex facial light transport, we factorize avatar synthesis into an animatable 3D intrinsic Gaussian proxy and a causal autoregressive neural shader. This proxy disentangles canonical geometry and reflectance attributes, rapidly rasterizing deformed Gaussians into view- and motion-consistent PBR buffers.
